## Jayapataka Svāmi, GBC-Eastern India and Bangladesh

Vyāsa-pūjā Offering to His Divine Grace Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda: The Transcendental Physician for the Modern Day

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

The bona fide spiritual master is accepted as good as Kṛṣṇa because he is the representative of Kṛṣṇa. This is confirmed in all Vedic scriptures and by all authorities. If one judges a spiritual master by his appearance, by his possessions, or by other superficial methods, the judgement will be imperfect. A spiritual master speaks from the transcendental platform the vibrations he has received from his spiritual master through disciplic succession. These transcendental words are known as *śabda-brahma* and cannot be compared with ordinary sounds. Just as a touchstone cannot be identified conclusively until it transforms base metal into gold, or a physician until he can cure his patient, similarly, a bona fide spiritual master is able to transform a disciple, even of inferior nature, into a first-class person, i.e., a devotee of the Lord, A spiritual master is not merely a pet or a fashion to maintain for some time and discard on a whim. The spiritual master is Kṛṣṇa’s way for coming to us and guiding us back to home, back to Godhead. It is serious business.

Knowledge is the light by which the darkness of ignorance is vanquished. Knowledge implies real knowledge, or knowledge free from error. Any kind of knowledge can be obtained in one of two ways. One way is by experimental knowledge, which is compared to blind men feeling the different limbs of an elephant and giving their postulations on the form and nature of the elephant. The blind man holding the trunk thinks and elephant is a gigantic snake, and the man holding the leg thinks another way. In this way different theories are evolved from different angles of observation, with no angle being complete or correct. The other method is by receiving knowledge by the descending process, i.e., through *guru-paramparā,* or disciplic succession. Knowledge handed down from the source of all knowledge, or descending knowledge, is perfect and free form defect because it doesn’t depend on the imperfect perceptions of imperfect people. Just as the sun is self-illuminating, real knowledge from Kṛṣṇa is self-effulgent and doesn’t depend on empirical speculation.

Considering the unique position of the spiritual master, it is essential to consider how one should approach him. *Bhagavad-gitā* says:

*tad viddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā*  
*upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*  
*(Bg 4.34)*

One must approach the spiritual master in a humble frame of mind, surrender unto him and offer him service. Then one should inquire about the truth from the spiritual master because he has seen the truth. This forms a reciprocal relationship of service and enlightenment between the *guru* and the disciple. In a challenging attitude one cannot get enlightenment from the spiritual master. Surrender means complete surrender without considering the momentary material profit or loss.

Everyone is looking for a friend—someone to love and trust. But in this material world if we put our trust in anyone, laying our head in his lap, he will cut our head off. This is the position. His Divine Grace Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda is showing how one can be a real well-wisher for everyone. Śrīla Prabhupāda is relieving everyone, without discrimination, from all kinds of dangerous conditions. Actually, everyone is constantly suffering—at every moment we are suffering—so much so it is taken for granted. No one questions why he is being harassed and troubled by other creatures, why he is being agitated by his body and mind, why he is growing old or dying. Neither taking the miseries of life for granted nor the cures of the superficial symptoms will help. The expert physician searches out the root cause and treats it; subsequently, all symptoms disappear. The spiritual master is the total physician of our composite disease of material life and its miseries. Śrīla Prabhupāda is curing his disciples and giving them real healthy life to lead in this life and the next. A normal doctor may demand high personal fees, but Śrīla Prabhupāda is asking nothing for himself. Everything is used or the satisfaction of Kṛṣṇa, and to uplift others from their miserable condition.

A patient’s relationship with his physician may end with the bill. But the spiritual master’s relationship with his patient (disciple) is eternal. Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura in his *Prayers to the Spiritual Master* describes how the spiritual master uplifts the devotee and trains him in this life, and in the liberated stage continues to guide the disciple to serving Kṛṣṇa with spiritual senses. On the other hand, if the disciple doesn’t finish his business and reach Kṛṣṇa in this life, the spiritual master again takes birth in this material world to deliver the disciple. The relationship between the *guru* and disciple is individual and everlasting. It is most nectarine and sweet.

Considering the great risk the spiritual master is taking for his disciple and the great gift he is giving, every disciple must take seriously his obligation to his spiritual master. Our relationship with Śrīla Prabhupāda is not one-sided. We have sufficient instructions from Śrīla Prabhupāda through his books and personal lectures and instructions to understand what is required of us to satisfy his desires. Śrīla Prabhupāda has done so much for all of us, we should try to finish our business and not compel him to come again.

Śrīla Prabhupāda is establishing truth from the *Vedas.* Recently Śrīla Prabhupāda said: “In this world there are two groups. One group is good. One group is bad. They are fighting with each other for supremacy. Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma are standing aside and watching both groups. We belong to Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma’s group.” Only Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma’s party is never defeated. (The whole world believed in the moon hoax, but Śrīla Prabhupāda stood firm. Now the cheating is being revealed…)

Out of thousands and thousands of men, a few are surrendering in devotion. As Śrīla Prabhupāda’s disciples our close connection must be maintained. How glorious, how rare are the devotees of Śrīla Prabhupāda! ISKCON is a transcendental hospital, curing all inmates of the material disease. Not everyone is cured upon admittance. It takes time. Day by day, moment by moment, according to sincerity and practice, the devotees are progressing. Any superficial symptoms of previous diseases are not very important in comparison with the fact of taking shelter of Śrīla Prabhupāda. Let us concentrate on the goal and consolidate our efforts to satisfy Śrīla Prabhupāda, spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the welfare of the whole world. Remember, a great soul sees only the good quality in a person, while an envious person sees only the bad qualities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s program is worldwide. Broad-minded persons can take part and feel their significance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness’ massive program. Śrīla Prabhupāda is our physician, and he is administering the cure. Chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, taking *mahā-prasāda* and working cooperatively with our Godbrothers to serve Śrī Kṛṣṇa and his devotees. The change of heart that the disciple undergoes under the guidance of the bona fide spiritual master is the proof of the effectiveness of the sincerity of the disciple. Śrīla Prabhupāda has already proved his efficacy, and now it is up to us to accept the cure and the medicine properly to perfect our lives and give this to others. This is Śrīla Prabhupāda’s desire. On this holy Vyāsa-pūjā day let us offer Śrīla Prabhupāda our obeisances and glorify his name and instructions. I beg to take the dust of the feet of the great souls who have taken as their life and soul the service of Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Jayapataka Svāmi  
GBC-Eastern India & Bangladesh

## Jayatīrtha dāsa, GBC-United Kingdom, Midwest USA

Please accept my most unworthy obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. The history books are filled with tales of man’s adventures, his journeys east and west in search of new frontiers, with their promises of wealth, wisdom and fountains of youth. In such vain search, man, even today, is climbing to the tops of mountains and diving to the depths of oceans. Let them look here and there to find what they will, but as far as I am concerned, all my searches have found their treasured goal in your lotus feet. As Dhruva Mahārāja prays:

*nūnaṁ vimuṣṭa-matayas tava māyayā te*  
*ye tvāṁ bhavāpyaya-vimokṣaṇam anya-hetoḥ*  
*arcanti kalpaka-taruṁ kuṇapopabhogyam*  
*icchanti yat sparśajaṁ niraye 'pi nṝṇām*  
*(Bhāg. 4.9.9)*

Although time wears away my youth with its fabled inevitability and my experience of the things of this world grows year by year, each Vyāsa-pūjā finds me all the more convinced that the three worlds and all their contents, when not engaged in the service of your lotus feet, are as useless as the stool of a boar. Although the measure of my conviction may increase, the foolish hobgoblins of my mind and senses still seek to push me away from your shelter. Although the whole world stands about me shipwrecked, why do I still look there for refuge? Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because of your Divine Grace only, there is still joy in the world, there is still hope in the world, there is still some future worth living for, some work worth doing, some words worth saying. Because of Your Divine Grace, man’s search still has a chance of reaching a worthwhile conclusion. I think, therefore, that because of your continued presence on earth, the sun and moon still rise at their appointed hours, the cows still give milk and the Deities in the temple shine forth in all Their Splendour.

The whole world is deluded by impersonalism out of frustration because in our experience all persons have exploited us and cheated us, and know that I am also a cheater. But in you we have found a true friend and well-wisher, and we can see by your example what a person is really meant to be. So we worship your lotus feet and pray for your benediction. As Prahlāda Mahāraja prays in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*:

*svasty astu viśvasya khalaḥ prasīdatāṁ*  
*dhyāyantu bhūtāni śivaṁ mitho dhiyā*  
*manaś ca bhadraṁ bhajatād adhokṣaje*  
*āveśyatāṁ no matir apy ahaitukī*

**“May there be good fortune throughout the universe, and may all envious persons be pacified. May all living entities become calm by practicing** ***bhakti-yoga,*** **for by accepting devotional service they will think of each other’s welfare. Therefore let us all engage in the service of the supreme transcendence, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and always remain absorbed in thought of Him.” (*****Bhāg.*** **5.18.9)**

We pray also that the whole world may become attracted to your message: that we are the tiny servants of the Absolute Supreme Personality of Godhead and that they may see that it is only by receiving the pure, unalloyed mercy of accepting Your Divine Grace as their spiritual master and saviour that their useless lives will gain value and meaning.

Please always give me this power of recollection. Please always keep me engaged in your service.

*Your most fallen disciple & servant,*

Jayatīrtha dāsa  
GBC-United Kingdom, Midwest USA

## Kīrtanānda Svāmī, GBC-Central North America

*Dear Śrila Prabhupāda,*

In the first chapter of *Ādi-līlā* in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* you have written, “It is not possible for a conditioned soul to directly meet Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, but if one becomes a sincere devotee and seriously engages in devotional service, Lord Kṛṣṇa sends an instructing spiritual master to show him favor and invoke his dormant propensity for serving the Supreme.”

Your mercy is incalculable because you have come to us, who were never serious and had no contact whatsoever with devotional service. You described us nicely in your arrival prayer: “Absorbed in material life, they think themselves very happy and satisfied, and therefore they have no taste for the transcendental message of Vāsudeva.” But just as Lord Caitanya engaged event the monkeys in Jhārikhaḍā forest in chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, you have come and engaged us *mlecchas* and *yavanas* in dancing and singing about Kṛṣṇa.

You have become glorious by carrying out the desire of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda; you are satisfied by virtue of your love for him and for Kṛṣṇa. There is nothing we can do to glorify you further. Our words are all inappropriate; our activities are so imperfect. Our only hope is that someday, by the potency of the Holy Name, we may become purified to the point of living as you have taught us. Then you, all the members of the disciplic succession, and Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself may become pleased to see all these fallen hippies dancing and singing just like devotees.

Kīrtanānda Svāmī  
GBC-Central North America

## Paṇcadraviḍa Svāmī, GBC-Latin America (Mexico, Central America, Colombia, Equador, Peru)

The light of your face, which glows with the golden hue of Lord Caitanya’s empowered mercy, shines, dazzling my eyes, and illuminates the only path of devotion. You are the knower of the unknown, Kṛṣṇa, who does not reveal Himself but to the most pure of fully surrendered souls. Because you bestow your mercy on all, your are more kind than kindness. You are sweeter than the fresh rose in spring, and once knowing you no one could ever seek shelter in this empty material world again. You are more tolerant than the most noble tree, and you only see what little good there may be in my service.

I fall down in the dust of your lotus feet again and again and beg you, “Please forgive me.” I am so proud, so vain. Let this demoniac pride be vanquished so that somehow or other I may begin to serve you.

Like a blind, stumbling dog following a munificent king, I am following you, hoping to lap up some scraps of mercy. In such a fallen state where is the question of service? Still you may kick me or reward me. That is your right because you are my only master. I know no other refuge than your benedicting feet.

*Your fallen servant,*

Paṇcadraviḍa Svāmī  
GBC-Latin America (Mexico, Central America, Colombia, Equador, Peru)

## Rameṣvara dāsa Svāmī, GBC-Western USA

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Every living being in the world today is in darkness, forced to struggle hard for nonpermanent goals and for relief from the continual onslaught of the miseries of material life. You have the power to free us from this oppressive bondage, and because you are a pure devotee of Lord Caitanya, you are imbued with the divine characteristic of unlimited mercy for the fallen souls. Your blessings have been received by hundreds of millions of people, and the entire course of history is being changed by your inconceivable powers and your pure love for Kṛṣṇa. Generally, “in this iron age of Kali men have but short lives. They are quarrelsome, lazy, misguided, unlucky and, above all, always disturbed.” (*Bhāg.* 1.1.10*)*Their health and life-span is reduced because of irregular habits, overeating, indulgence in sense gratification, overdependence on another’s mercy and artificial standards of living—all of which weaken and waste their vital energy. Their laziness especially refers to spiritual practices, due to illusory bodily misidentification. Because everyone’s mind is victimized by many diversions of politics, cinema, sports, gambling, clubs, mundane libraries, bad association, smoking, drinking, cheating, pilfering, quarrelling, etc., they are always disturbed and full of anxieties. The whole world atmosphere is surcharged with faithlessness, lying propaganda and the strain of competition for sense gratification, enmity and war. Despite external appearances, it is impossible for any person to independently achieve happiness, peace of mind, and freedom from the suffering inflicted by this distorted way of life.

When Raghunātha dāsa finally escaped from his family and achieved shelter at the lotus feet of Lord Caitanya, the Lord told him, “The mercy of Lord Kṛṣṇa is stronger than anything else. Therefore the Lord has delivered you from the ditch of materialistic life, which is like a hole into which people pass stool.” (Cc. *Antya* 6.193*)*Lord Caitanya emphasized that a life of material comfort is like a ditch of stool, and anyone addicted to the materialistic way of life is like a worm living in stool. Even though it is abominable, the worm cannot give up the stool, and similarly even though material life is wretched, the poor living entity cannot get out of this filthy entanglement. Only the mercy of Kṛṣṇa can save one from such misery!

At the beginning of the Kali-yuga, the sages at Naimiṣāranya, who had gathered to perform sacrifices to counteract the degrading influence of the age, questioned Sūta Gosvāmī how people would be able to receive the mercy of Kṛṣṇa since the Lord had left the world for His own transcendental abode. Sūta Gosvāmī answered that in this age the mercy of the Lord would be delivered to all people through transcendental books. “This *Bhāgavat Purānā* is as brilliant as the sun, and it has arisen just after the departure of Lord Kṛṣṇa to His own abode, accompanied by religion, knowledge, etc. Persons who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the age of Kali shall get light from this *Purānā.”*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your books have descended from the spiritual world and are full of the all-powerful potencies of the Personality of Godhead. They are nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa and contain all His mercy. When your disciples voluntarily approach the fallen souls to distribute these books, and engage them in giving a donation and taking a book, their lives become perfect because they have received the inconceivable mercy of the Lord. Simply by giving a donation or taking a book they have begun their favourable association with Your Divine Grace, the spiritual master of the universe, and they become infected, by your blessings, with divine, transcendental qualities, beginning with the affinity to hear about Kṛṣṇa, to associate with His devotees, etc. Anyone who engages in Lord Caitanya’s *saṇkīrtana* movement in this way receives your transcendental blessings because you are the ocean of mercy. In this way, millions of people are taking your books every year and are receiving the seed of devotional life and love for Kṛṣṇa. It appears externally that you are in India or America, but actually you are in the spiritual world, eternally bestowing your blessings on all who come in contact with you. You are so powerful that simultaneously millions of living entities in all different parts of the world can receive your blessings at once through these books. Who can know your real position or the extent of your power? It is said in the *Ādi-līlā* that, “The internal devotees or potencies are all eternal associates in the pastimes of the Lord. Only with them does the Lord advent to propound the *saṇkīrtana* movement, only with them does the Lord taste the mellow of conjugal love, and only with them does He distribute this love of God to people in general.” (*Ādi* 7.18-19) Lord Caitanya has sent you to this world to establish his mission and change the entire world order. All His desires are being fulfilled by you, and the earth planet is becoming a gateway for all the inhabitants of this universe to return to Kṛṣṇaloka. Just to receive your blessings the demigods on the heavenly planets are waiting in line to take birth here. This glorious day of your appearance in the world will be forever celebrated as the most significant transcendental event in the history of Lord Caitanya’s movement, for you have transformed the world’s *karma* and re-established spiritual life. In great awe and reverence I fall at your lotus feet and beg to be engaged by you in helping distribute your books in every village and town, for as long as you’ll allow me this privilege.

*Your unworthy servant,*

Rameṣvara dāsa Svāmī  
GBC-Western USA

## Rūpānuga dāsa, GBC, Mid-Eastern U.S., Canada, Trustee, Bhaktivedanta Institute

In 1942, Columbus sailed into the Atlantic, competing with other explorers to discover the easiest way to India, the land of wonderful spices, silks, tapestries, jewels, gold and silver. The feudal lords and barons of Europe wanted to add more taste to their venison. They desired finer fabrics, diamonds and emeralds to decorate their ladies. They required richer woven drapes for their dullish castle walls and precious metals to increase their wealth and power. Columbus landed at the Bahama Islands off the coast of Florida and, thinking that he had found India, called the natives “Indians.” He was in the vanguard of the many foolish men from the Western countries who have approached India for materialistic, external reasons. They never found the real treasure of India, hidden until Your Divine Grace revealed the complete science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in your books.

The British were supposedly able administrators, but they never cared for the real opulence, the storehouse of love of God. They were determined to “civilize” the subcontinent, in their illusion not seeing the Vedic culture but only confusing the mud and thatched huts of Bengal with those of tribal Africa. Yet, a few centuries before the British rule, Lord Caitanya and Prabhu Nityānanda danced in those simple huts at Māyāpur, in the company of the greatest Vaiṣṇāva saints, relishing the topmost internal pastimes of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. And these truths would still be secret if it wasn’t for the books of Your Divine Grace.

In 1492, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu was enjoying the pastimes of His *paugaṇḍa* age, during which His chief occupation was to engage Himself in study. Meanwhile, Columbus sailed into the womb of oblivion.

Today, while Western sceptics continue to flirt with *Māyāvādī* catastrophe, and hover over India’s external features—the so-called poverty, “over-population,” the caste system” and underdeveloped technology—Your Divine Grace proceeds to methodically conquer the culture of the Western world, without firing a shot, simply by distributing your books.

Those concerned with external history say this is the year 1977, but historians will know it as the year 491, Caitanya Era, the Age of the Hare Kṛṣṇa Movement. Personally, I have no legitimate expectation of going back to Godhead, but I can only pray to remain in this Movement birth after birth for the next 9,500 years. I beg to remain your ISKCON man.

*Your most fallen disciple,*

Rūpānuga dāsa, GBC  
Mid-Eastern U.S., Canada  
Trustee, Bhaktivedanta Institute

## Satsvarūpa dāsa Gosvāmī, GBC-Pacific Northwest, Dallas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*Namaḥ*-obeisances. Before you came, Śrīla Prabhupāda, there were no real obeisances. As children, we submitted to the guidance of our parents and society’s overseers, but as we grew up we rejected their leadership. I know in my own case I submissively imbibed allegiance to conventional religious belief and nation-worship—and later found the objects of obeisances to be false, hypocritical, blind. Some of us in hopelessness then made “obeisances” to marijuana, LSD, hippy life radical politics, or to more respectable material gods—but all were later exposed as cheaters. But in rendering utmost respect to you, we honor love and spiritual wisdom personified. Bowing down to you again and again is natural and proper. Why? Because you are the confidential servitor of Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Lord Kṛṣṇa’s greatness demands we make repeated prostrated obeisances when faced with His inconceivable, unlimited opulences as the Supreme Controller, the resting place of the innumerable souls and the source of all material and spiritual universes. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the proven representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa, and in making obeisances to you we honor and please Kṛṣṇa Himself. And because you alone have so kindly come and expertly taken us out of our abysmal suffering condition (*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka)* we personally thank you and bow at your feet, begging to be maintained by you in eternal devotional service to the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*oṁ-*address. As our spiritual master you have taught us how to address you and how to address the Supreme Personality of Godhead through you. Otherwise, as we did not know how to make obeisances, so we also did not know how to address you or the Lord. So you have given us this Śrīla Prabhupāda *praāti,* and it is completely right and fitting.

*viśṇu-pādāya-*unto him who is at the feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Because you are most humbly taking shelter at the feet of the Supreme Lord, therefore you are the greatest person in human society and therefore we follow your example and instructions. We consider the dust of your feet the most treasured crown. You do not act outside of Kṛṣṇa’s devotional service, and you always remain confident of the protection He extends to the surrendered soul. This is no mere figure of speech, but you have demonstrated your surrender as the boldest and *the most successful world-*ācārya *ever,* on behalf of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*kṛṣṇā-preṣṭhāya-*who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Because you are taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, therefore you are very dear to the transcendental Lord. One who is dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa is very rare (*sa mahātmā sudurlabhaḥ),* and he is like an auspicious touchstone who can turn whomever he meets into another rare devotee of Kṛṣṇa. You are speaking Kṛṣṇa’s eternal teachings throughout the world in millions of published books. Thousands of dedicated *bhaktas* are surrendered to you and engaged by you in full-time preaching of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s glories. You have induced the whole world into chanting “Hare Kṛṣṇa!” Anyone with intelligence can understand that you are *kṛṣṇā-preṣṭhāya,* very dear to Kṛṣṇa.

*bhū-tale-*on the earth. You are preaching here on the earth, the planet of death. Lord Kṛṣṇa sent you here. You came to 26 Second Avenue, New York City, the earth, and the East Village newspaper proclaimed your mission with the front-page headline: “Save Earth now!”. You are the saviour of the earth, and therefore you live amongst us, although you have nothing to do with the earth, being entirely spiritual. But because you are engaged in preaching you are moving all over this earth planet and informing us, “You are not this body, which is nothing but earth, water and fire. You are all eternal spirit souls; you belong to Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual world.” It is no exaggeration to say you bless the earth wherever your lotus feet touch.

*śrīmate-*all beautiful. This describes Lord Kṛṣṇa, and it is also the address preceding your name. You alone, out of so many *gurus* and *yogīs,* teachers and leaders, carry the eternally youthful beauty of Kṛṣṇa. The *Kṛṣṇa Book* describes that Devakī became the most beautiful to behold when Kṛṣṇa was residing in her womb before His birth. You also are pure and beautiful to behold, bearing Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name in the prisonlike atmosphere of 20thcentury Kali-yuga.

*Bhaktivedānta-svāmin-*A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami. Not just “gurudeva” we praise, or “the spiritual master,” but the one Śrīla Prabhupāda who is the object of our personal prayer and homage, the only one, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Keeping your name is our claim to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s attention. We will always go on chanting your name in Śrīla Prabhupāda *Praṇati*; your name is directly the holy name and can be chanted as such.

*iti nāmine-*who is named. Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*namaḥ-*obeisances. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories especially to his *vāṇī.* But all glories to his personal *darśana* as well, soft as a rose, and hard as the thunderbolt.

*te-*unto you. Our devotional service is rendered not to Lord Kṛṣṇa directly, but unto you. *Prasāda* means offering foodstuff first unto you. The vows of *brahmacarya* and *sannyāsa* are made unto you. *Gṛhasthas’* regulated promises are unto you; and their children are rendered unto you. Offerings of life, money, intelligence and words are unto you, and the entire *saṇkīrtana* movement is rendering service unto you-every book and every dollar collected is rendered unto you. And because you are *viśṇu-pādāya,* we know for certain that whatever we render to you goes lovingly directed to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. There is no Kṛṣṇa *prema* unless it is *bhakti* unto you.

*sārasvate deve-*servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī; spiritual master. We have heard the greatness of our grandfather spiritual master, your *guru mahārāja.* He was a lion-*guru*, and on his order you came to the West and saved us. Here you are impressing on us that you are the meek servitor of your spiritual master. You ask us to pray to you in your connection with Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvamī. You are our lord and master and yet you are the servant of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvamī. “Whatever little success I have achieved,” you’ve told us, is coming from your believing a hundred percent in the words of your spiritual master. You have complete faith in Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Prabhupāda. We cannot understand your intimate relationship with your spiritual master, but we aspire to worship you in your obedience to his order. That you are his servant is also the secret of *our* success; we want to help you carry out the order of your *guru-mahārāja,* Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and thus please him.

*gaura-vāī*-the message of Lord Caitanya. This is the substance of your teaching. Scholars may say Caitanya Mahāprabhu was a great Vaiśāva saint of long ago, five hundred years past, but He is not still completely with us as we push on Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṇkīrtana* movement on your order? Yes, Caitanya Mahaprabhu is living in this Hare Kṛṣṇa Movement—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛsṇā Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare. You are delivering His message undiluted, and it is being appreciated more and more by young men who are joining you, as well as by learned scholars, as the highest contribution to all living entities. And you have demonstrated in your seventeen volumes of *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* with forcible logic and Vedic evidence, that Caitanya Mahāprabhu is Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself, the cause of all causes.

*pracāriṇe-*who are preaching. You have well earned retirement millions of times, but you go on preaching. Sometimes you are so weak you cannot stand, you cannot eat, but you go on preaching. “Help the suffering humanity. They are all in ignorance and cannot understand that there is another life. Give them Kṛṣṇa.” It is only rascaldom if we claim not to understand our duty as your disciples. I heard you myself at one initiation, as you handed the *sannyāsīs* their *daḍas:* “Now preach! Preach! Go preach!”

*nirviśeśa-*from impersonalism; *śūnya-vādi-*from voidism. Previously we did not even know the disease, although we were inflicted. Now we are cured and equipped to fight all kinds of Māyāvādīs. You have trained us all expertly. “I am simply praying,” you have said, “for the mercy to be able to fight on behalf of Caitanya Mahāprabhu.”

*pāścātya;* western; *deśa-*countries. The further west you go in India, people are most like asses, said Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Nārada Muni described the western gate of the body as the source of degradation through unrestrained sexual proclivity. But now Kṛṣṇa consciousness is firmly implanted in the West. “The brainwashing books are already there.” It is spreading like an epidemic. We can eventually see the day when the West becomes pious under Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The blind man and the lame man are cooperating. It is all because of your preaching, on the order of Lord Caitanya and Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī.

*tāriṇe-*who are delivering. Please engage us always in your sublime devotional service. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Satsvarūpa dāsa Gosvāmī  
GBC-Pacific Northwest, Dallas

## Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa, GBC, Minister of Higher Education

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Your Divine Grace, my lord and master, has shown most scientifically that the fundamental principle of knowledge is to know the difference between *matter* and *spirit,* that is, between the field of activities and the knower of the field, *kṣetra-kṣetrajṇayor jṇānam* (Bg. 13.3). It is your divine mercy and priceless gift that we are blessed to see that matter per se is characterized by low information-content. It can be transformed into different complex forms, sizes, colors, tastes, fragrance, etc., of high information-content by the touch of the spirit (life) just like a potter can mold clay into different forms for different purposes. Missing this very basic point, the material scientists are guided by *māyā,* the illusory energy of the Divine Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, *māyayāpahṛta-jṇānāḥ* (Bg. 7.15). So they take shelter of catch words like “chance,” “molecular evolution,” “mutation,” etc., which can be shown to have no scientific validity whatsoever.

You have also demonstrated that the ultimate goal of all knowledge is to know Vāsudeva, Lord Kṛṣṇa, *vāsudeva-parā vedāḥ…(Bhāg.* 1.2.28*),*and the nature of the Absolute Truth must be the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You have proved without any tinge of doubt that *bhakti* is the only scientific process by which one can know the actual features of the Absolute Truth and is indeed the very science of the relationship between *jīvātma* and Paramātmā, which is the basis of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and the goal of life.

How fortunate is this most fallen soul to have been allowed by the all-merciful Lord Hari to have *darśana* of your lotus feet. Your instructions are so powerful-like the echoing thunder of the monsoon season. “What good is your Ph.D. unless it is used for Kṛṣṇa?” you remarked oftentimes. Your glance is full of compassion and loving care. Remembering your divine instructions constantly makes me fearless like a child in the lap of his mother. Born most lowly, I never know how to behave and speak before Your Divine Grace. You tolerated and forgave all my offenses. There is no one more merciful than you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please allow me to prostrate myself before the dust of your lotus feet birth after birth.

*Most fallen servant,*

Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa  
GBC, Minister of Higher Education

## Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Gosvāmī, Personal Secretary to Śrīla Prabhupāda, GBC-Northeast USA, China

All glories, all glories  
To our beloved lord,  
Who appeared in lokanātha Mullick’s home  
By his own sweet accord.

Jagannātha in procession  
With young friends he did play;  
Now introduced throughout the world-  
Ratha-yātrā Day.

Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa arcane  
From his father he did imbibe,  
Installed Them in all nations,  
Mankind purified.

Kṛṣṇṣ consciousness movement,  
Your banner is unfurled.  
Mahāprabhu’s message  
Now over floods the world.

Your books, praised by scholars,  
Act as cintāmaī stones,  
Create Vaikunṭha atmosphere  
In all your reader’s homes.

Bhagavad-gītā As It Is  
Kṛṣṇa unalloyed,  
Speculators, interpreters,  
Cheaters-all destroyed.

You condemn them without exception,  
No one dares reply:  
“Anthill civilization,  
Śva-viḍ-varāhośṭṛa-kharaiḥ.”

Māyāpur’s planetarium,  
Vṛndāvana Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma,  
Bombay science joins culture  
Broadcasting hari-nāma.

Blessed are the devotees  
Who shelter at thy Feet,  
A life imbued with bhakti  
Kṛṣṇa surely meet.

Your Grace, like the sun  
Illuminates the day,  
Blind, a fallen soul,  
Your mercy I do pray.

Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Gosvāmī  
Personal Secretary to Śrīla Prabhupāda  
GBC-Northeast USA, China

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from ISKCON centers around the world

## Adelaide

*Gurudeva kṛpā-bindu diyā*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the most dear servant of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura because, in spite of all personal difficulties, you have taken his order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world as your life and soul. You are showing us by your example how to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Give everything to the spiritual master and the spiritual master will give you everything-Kṛṣṇa. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are so fallen and you are an immeasurable ocean of mercy. We are the most fallen because you have given us the truth and we still reject it. O how unfortunate we are, O Gurudeva. The most sinful man can become Kṛṣṇa conscious simply by a small drop of your mercy—simply by your wish. From Lord Kṛṣṇa’s abode you have come to this hell. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, even though we are so unwilling, please drag us back to Kṛṣṇa!

*Your aspiring servants,* Adelaide Mandira

Vaṁśīvadana Suvāsā dāsa brahmacārī / Nirabhadra dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Peter / Sarva Lakśaṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Patrick / Gaura Maṇḍala Bhūmi dāsa brahmacārī / Pūrṇacandra dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Michael / Rāsamaṇḍala-devī dāsī / Veṇu Gopāla dāsa brahmācarī / Bhakta John / Dāmodara

## Ahmedabad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We are indeed proud of offering our humble obeisances unto your most exalted lotus feet. When Jesus Christ was on this earth, he had only a handful of sincerely dedicated disciples, but because he was a genuine master, now Christianity is spread all over the world. We have now no doubt that the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is going to sweep across the entire universe hundreds of times more than Christianity. This is because not only are you the most genuine *ācārya*—representative of God—but you are the most daring devotee. You have truly staked everything to spread the message of Kṛṣṇa. You have not hidden any secrets. You have boldly declared the supremacy of Kṛṣṇa in a place where they had hardly heard about Him. When every other Vaiṣṇava Godbrother of yours was afraid of dirtying his hands to pick up the fallen souls, you picked up the challenge and put your hands in the most foul muck of the Western materialistic existence. As a result, thousands of impure creatures like us are stuck to your lotus hand. Just as the fish which is flapping in the sand gets relief when put back in water, or a traveller in the desert gets new life when he sees a reservoir of water, we have found our home in your fearless shelter.

Your mercy is unlimited and all-pervading. Once you told us, “Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī has warned that the spiritual master should not take too many disciples, but I am still taking many disciples, because if I don't where will they go? What will be their fate?" This mercy of yours is your unique quality. Actually, because of taking so many sinful people as disciples, your health has deteriorated. You have no *karmas*to pay for, but since you are accepting the sinful reactions of your fallen disciples, who sometimes go back to their old sinful habits, you are suffering. Of course, you are above any suffering because you are absorbed in the thoughts of Kṛṣṇa, but your sickness breaks our hearts. Recently when Your Divine Grace was sick in Māyāpur, one night your body was burning with high fever, and I was trying to massage you with the broken heart, grief and anxiety, being unable to take away your fever. But you all of a sudden started speaking: "Why are these rascal scientists saying life comes from matter?" and another time you asked, "What do they say when we say everything belongs to Kṛṣṇa?" So even in the midst of heavy fever you are only thinking how to deliver the fallen souls and how to save them from the atheistic propaganda which is going on everywhere. You are actually showing us how to be perfect Vaiṣṇavas. Even though you are the spiritual master of the entire universe, everyone thinks you are his friend. You are not taking any credit for all your achievements. You are just saying, "It is all because of the grace of my guru-mahārāja," and whatever remaining credit, you give to your disciples. "Because you are all serving me so nicely, everything is going on nicely." Once someone was saying in Māyāpur when the temple was completed, "Now Śrīla Prabhupāda’s desire is fulfilled." Then you immediately said, "Not my desire. It was Bhaktivinoda Thākura's desire that Bengali devotees and Western devotees shall dance together glorifying the holy name of the Lord. So now his desire has come true. Who am I?" You always enthuse your disciples with your ever-dynamic preaching ideas. Your desire is to see that every human being becomes Kṛṣṇa conscious. Just as Vāsudeva Datta was willing to take over the sinful reactions of all the people of the world so that they may become eternally happy, you also want to make everyone happy by making him Kṛṣṇa conscious at the cost of anything.

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are a thunderbolt for the *bhogī yogīs*and Māyāvādīs, but for your devotees you are like a rose petal. Somehow or other we have now smelled the rose of your lotus feet and gained the life eternal, full of bliss and knowledge. Please give us the grace and strength so that we may stick to your lotus feet and continue this most exalted mission that you have started.

We are very sincerely praying at your lotus feet that you may get whatever health we have and the remaining years of our useless lives. One moment of your being on this earth is worth hundreds of lifetimes of ignorant fools like us. So please stay on this earth and remain the shelter for thousands of afflicted living beings.

Your qualities are unlimited, and only Śukadeva Gosvāmī or Lord Śiva or Nārada can glorify you properly. We are contaminated and ignorant dogs of yours. How much can we glorify you?

*cakhu-dāna dilā jei, janme janme prabhu sei*  
*divya-jñāna hṛde prakāśita*  
*prema-bhakti jāhā haite, abidyā bināśa jāte*  
*bede gāya jāhāra carita*

He who has given me the gift of transcendental vision is my lord, birth after birth. By his mercy, divine knowledge is revealed within the heart, bestowing *prema-bhakti* and destroying ignorance. The Vedic scriptures sing of his character.

Please forgive our countless offenses and bestow your causeless mercy. We prostrate at your lotus feet again and again.

*Your fallen servants at ISKCON Ahmedabad*

Yaśomatinandana dāsa adhikārī / Kṛṣṇanāma dāsa brahmacārī / Radhakunda-devī dāsī / Devadharma dāsa brahmacārī / Upendra Muni Prabhu / Sundarī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇavallabha dāsa brahmacārī / Kumāra Prabhu / Karuṇāmaya dāsa brahmacārī / Jagdīśa Prabhu

## Amsterdam

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Formerly, we had no desire for becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and were shamelessly serving the unlimited orders of lusty desires, without even wanting to give them up. But now you have awakened our spiritual intelligence and taught us to disobey the demands of *māyā*. Now we are feeling somewhat relieved, having realized what a dangerous predicament we were in. We were just like blind men walking on a cliffside with jagged rocks below. We think, therefore, that you have saved our lives; indeed, you have saved us from unlimited suffering through millions of lives. What can we say or do, therefore, to express our gratitude? Certainly we cannot ever repay this debt to you. By your mercy the gateway to liberation has been opened.

*Mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimukteḥ*, we pray that we may follow your instructions without deviation or concoction and thus not cause you pain. You are kindly allowing us to assist you in the worship of Śrī Śrī Rādhā Gopinātha and in spreading the mission of Lord Caitanya. Without your mercy the whole world would have gone to hell, but, by your presence, millions will be saved.

How can we estimate our good fortune to be living on the same planet, which has become a *tīrtha* by the touch of your lotus feet, what to speak of actually being members of your movement? Certainly we must be amongst the most fortunate souls in the universe.

ISKCON Amsterdam

## Ann Arbor

**"Again there are those who, although not conversant in spiritual knowledge, begin to worship the Supreme Person upon hearing about Him from others. Because of their tendency to hear from authorities, they also transcend the path of birth and death." (*****Bg*****. 13.26)**

The essence of religion can be enunciated only by the Personality of Godhead Himself. Kṛṣṇa knows everything perfectly and the pure devotee is only speaking exactly what Kṛṣṇa speaks. Therefore, to hear from him means to receive the unadulterated Absolute Truth. When Kṛṣṇa is known all things become known, and that understanding illuminates everything as the sun lights up the day. Like dark and light, side by side coexisting, the surrendered soul experiences sublime life even in this world (*brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā* Bg. 18.54) while the mundaner is always filled with anxieties and grief. The simple difference is the speck in the eye, the arrogant refusal to see the world as designed for Kṛṣṇa's pleasure.

This perspective is the gift of the bona fide spiritual master. He does not take the risks and burdens of the material world merely to deliver some temporary trinket of mystic *siddhis*, or a coyly turned rationale for dog and hog life. He is delivering eternal life, full of bliss and knowledge—nothing less—to all who are willing to strive for it. Because the pure devotee has actually seen the truth, he can bring the haze of ambiguous terms-Supreme, absolute, liberation, oneness—into the crystal—clear focus of ever-youthful Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, surrounded by eternal loving servitors in the transcendental land of *cintāmaṇi*.

Therefore our obligation is clear-salvation delivered in exchange for sincere service. As that father invests time and trouble in an infant child with the hope that the child will become a good son later to assist him, so we are always praying to become mature sons and daughters able to assist in maintaining and increasing Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana*movement. May we always feel indebted.

Your eternal servants,

Badarinārāyaṇa dāsa / Bhakta Eric / Amara-devī dāsī / Yamunātaṭacara dāsa / Bhakta Lonnie / Devala-devī dāsī / Mitravān dāsa / Bhakta Tim / Nīla Mādhava-devī dāsī / Paripūrṇa dāsa / Navasī-devī dāsī / Anasūyā-devī dāsī / Vaṁśi dāsa / Dhenumatī-devī dāsī

## Atlanta

By nature's own way we have all been bewildered.  
In every step vexed, through this world we meandered

Without proper purpose in so many species.  
Blind fools, our own suffering we saw but did not see

Your fixed lotus feet offering shelter to all.  
Grieving, waiting in hell for your mercy to fall,

Bereft of your guidance to life's real purpose.  
Confused, nay, perplexed-and of God hardly curious-

How could souls such as we ever know something higher  
Than lamenting and hankering-without your desire?

*Brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-Kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*  
*Cc. Madhya 19.151*

Guru Mahārāja, you have given us the seed of life, the priceless science of *bhakti*. Please look upon us favorably as we attempt to water the seed, for if you are not enlivened by our service then we are only dead bodies, having no value to anyone.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are of all value to everyone; a transparent via medium for Kṛṣṇa's mercy, revealing real life where before was only nescience. Save the whole world, Prabhupāda, somehow or other please save them all.

Please accept us among your foot servants and order carriers.

**New Panihati**

## Auckland

Before attempting to glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us first offer our heads at your lotus feet. By the power of our minds, and by our so-called knowledge, we can not properly see or understand you; how then can we hope to offer you the praise that you really deserve? It is not possible. Our only hope, therefore, of presenting a nice offering to you on this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā is if, by your mercy, we can give up (if even for a moment) the ascending method of approach, and witness our own frailties and impurities. In such a state of humility, one may see just how far away from Kṛṣṇa he actually is and thereby simultaneously perceive how close to Kṛṣṇa you are. Only in such a state of utter humility and love is it actually possible to offer you sincere praise. Before continuing, let us apologize that we have not come to this condition. Lacking such humility, our offering will surely be laced with deceit and pretence. It will certainly be childish and lacking in sincerity. We hope you will forgive us for this.

The spiritual master is the mercy manifestation of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. As such he can never be considered separate or different from Kṛṣṇa. If one is fortunate enough to meet such a spiritual master, it is as good as meeting Kṛṣṇa.

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

Thus we are very fortunate to have your association, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for associating with the pure devotee is as good as associating with Kṛṣṇa. Similarly, your instructions are nondifferent from the instructions of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, for you are His external manifestation.

Because of our lamentable condition, and owing to the fact that we find it difficult to utilize anything other than our senses in the pursuit of knowledge, Kṛṣṇa has sent you to us. You are a liberated devotee; you are a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra*, an empowered incarnation of Kṛṣṇa Himself, appearing only for the benefit of the most fallen souls.

**"Since one cannot visually experience the presence of the Supersoul, He appears before us as a liberated devotee. Such a spiritual master is no one other than Kṛṣṇa Himself." (*****Cc. Ādi līlā*** **1.53)**

Your mercy is distributed in the form of transcendental sound, and by hearing you speak and by receiving your orders we have incurred a debt that can never really be repaid. The burden of such a debt is not really a burden, however; it is a great joy, for the spiritual doorway has been opened. Now it is up to us to enter or not to enter. The only repayment we can make for taking these instructions is to act on them. This is the open secret of transcendental life. The spiritual master can be pleased by one who follows his instructions, and not otherwise. Our great hope is that someday we may be able to actually please you with body, mind and words. This is the way in which a sincere disciple can genuinely honor or glorify his spiritual master. This Vyāsa-pūjā offering is like a tiny candle offered to a great sun, and we are ashamed that it could not be nicer.

from your humble servants,  
Hare Kṛṣṇa Marae and North Shore

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. You are always absorbed in the thoughts of Lord Kṛṣṇa and cannot forget Him even for a moment.

*mac-cittā mad-gata-prāṇā bodhayantaḥ parasparam*  
*kathayantaś ca māṁ nityaṁ tuṣyanti ca ramanti ca*

**"The thoughts of My pure devotees dwell in Me, their lives are surrendered to Me, and they derive great satisfaction and bliss enlightening one another and conversing about Me." (Bg. 10.9)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, everything you do and say and plan is as good as Kṛṣṇa's plan. Therefore you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are very victorious and successful in your preaching mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You are a true *mahātmā*, always glorifying the Lord.

*satataṁ kīrtayanto māṁ yatantaś ca dṛḍha-vratāḥ*  
*namasyantaś ca māṁ bhaktyā nitya-yuktā upāsate*

**"Always chanting My glories, endeavoring with great determination, bowing down before Me, these great souls perpetually worship Me with devotion." (Bg. 9.14)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you can make this whole world God conscious and make everyone servant of Kṛṣṇa instead of servant of the senses, because the embodied soul, by his immemorial desire to avoid Kṛṣṇa consciousness, causes his own bewilderment. Due to this he forgets his constitutional position as eternal, blissful servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*tad viddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā*  
*upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*

**"Just try to learn the truth by approaching a bona fide spiritual master. Inquire from him submissively and render service unto him. The self-realized soul can impart knowledge unto you because he has seen the truth." (Bg. 4.34)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, by inquiring submissively and rendering service like a menial servant without false prestige and satisfying you is the secret of advancement in spiritual life. Inquiries and submission constitute the proper combination for spiritual understanding. Unless there is submission and service and inquiries to the spiritual master it will not be effective. Therefore, when the student is submissive and always ready to render service, the reciprocation of knowledge and inquiries becomes perfect.

*api ced asi pāpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ pāpa-kṛt-tamaḥ*  
*sarvaṁ jñāna-plavenaiva vṛjinaṁ santariṣyasi*

**"Even if you are considered to be the most sinful of all sinners, when you are situated on the boat of transcendental knowledge you will be able to cross over the ocean of miseries." (Bg. 4.36)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we, as your disciples, would like to become very disciplined to your instructions and become your menial servants. Even though we are so conditioned in our existence, we pray that you may accept our insignificant service so that we may purify our existence to cross the dangerous ocean of Kali-yuga.

*tvaṁ naḥ sandarśito dhātrā*  
*dustaraṁ nistitīrṣatām*  
*kaliṁ sattva-haraṁ puṁsāṁ*  
*karṇa-dhāra ivārṇavam*

**“We think that we have met Your Goodness by the will of providence, just so that we may accept you as captain of the ship for those who desire to cross the difficult ocean of Kali, which deteriorates all the good qualities of a human being.”** ***(Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **1.1.22)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been indulging in so much sense gratification for so many hundreds and thousands of years, and finally by the grace of Lord Kṛṣṇa we have come in contact with a pure devotee like yourself, Śrīla Prabhupāda!!

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*  
*(Cc. Madhya 19.151)*

**"Upon traversing this whole material creation of Lord Brahmā, one fortunate soul might get the** ***bhakti-latā*** **(seed of devotion). This happens by the grace of guru and Kṛṣṇa."**

Your eternal servants,

Aṣṭasiddhi dāsa adhikārī / Vasusveśa dāsa brahmacārī / Rāmaṇīya-devi dāsī / Sahadeva dāsa adhikārī / Vrajarāja dāsa brahmacārī / Rādhikā Ramaṇa-devī dāsī / Pārvatī dāsa adhikārī / Vinoda dāsa brahmacārī / Bhaktin Cilia / Bhūtanātha dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Mike / Bhaktin Janice / Uṣāpati dāsa brahmacārī / Jñānamūrti-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Ricky

## Australia BBT TSKP

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Hare Kṛṣṇa! Please accept our most humble obeisances and our prostrated selves in the dust of your lotus feet. We beg to offer you our service for the rest of eternity, and the following prayer for your pleasure to commemorate the most blessed event for all: your appearance day in this world.

**Eight Prayers Glorifying the Spiritual Master**

Invocation:

Ordained was the time in '65  
when His Divine Grace first came  
with Bhāgavatams, $7 and sweet hari-nāma,  
our lives were never the same.

You came to us with our eyes shut tight;  
delivering souls is your mission.  
illuminating our ignorance with knowledge so bright  
you freed us from māyā's condition.

You gave us the choice to serve māyā or God,  
and going 'Back Home' is the goal,  
prema-bhakti to Kṛṣṇa, the means and the end  
only service to you is the toll.

"Distribute my books! Distribute my books,  
distribute my books to please me!"  
and pleasure to you is pleasure to Kṛṣṇa.  
Life's meant for that opportunity.

Our previous lives, inexperienced by years,  
that moment you came was our start,  
now saṅkīrtana is our 'life and soul',  
your instructions engraved in our hearts.

With determination, enthusiasm and patience we'll fight  
Māyā's onslaughts, with you at command;  
You 're like Hanumān bridging the gap to Śrī Laṅkā,  
we're small spiders just kicking the sand.

O eternal father, please bestow upon us  
sincere service; so our Lord we can meet,  
after drowning so long in this ocean of death  
help us cling to your sweet lotus feet!!

Blessed's the day when Śrī Kṛṣṇa appeared,  
Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya and you, and we pray  
the day you return to cintāmaṇi-dhāma  
we'll stay dāsas to you, our prabhu.

Australian BBT-TSKP

## Baltimore

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen, worthless obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. You are the most glorious personality and you are the savior of the entire world. From your lotus mouth showers the transcendental rain of Lord Caitanya's pastimes of mercy, which destroys all the ignorance of this material world.

When this nectar mixes with the flood of devotional service, emanating just like moonbeams from your lotus feet, an ocean of Kṛṣṇa *prema* surges forth. Every place that Your Divine Grace's lotus feet set down unlock the floodgate of this love of Kṛṣṇa, and all the living entities there become drowned in the holy name of the Lord. Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare.

We fall at your feet again and again. You are the supreme friend of everyone and you are the *ācārya* of the three worlds.

We are lowly, filthy, rotten and the most contaminated entities, and you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the only hope for such weak-willed and evil-hearted souls as us. We are madly engaged in chewing the chewed and sinking deeper and deeper into the hell of material life, completely and grossly covered with illusion.

You are so kind, tolerant, expert and merciful that you are repeatedly saving us again and again from the burning stench of this illusion.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your golden face is just like the rising sun, and from your golden heart emanates the moonlight of compassion. When your golden lotus lips narrate the ripened fruit and essence of transcendental knowledge, you open our eyes and hearts to the eternal beauty and bliss of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We are now afraid of this material world. We don't want to take birth here anymore and work hard like an ass, foaming at the mouth, full of frustration and anxiety, and always being defeated by death: *pa, pha. ba, bha, ma.*

All this just to chew the chewed.

You are our only shelter and you have so mercifully saved us from this eternal hell.

You have given us the chance to be eternally young, blissful, and full of knowledge, just as you are. All fear of life is gone when we remember Your Divine Grace. When we chant Hare Kṛṣṇa we feel ourselves to be the most fortunate people in the universe.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, being your insignificant servant is better than any other position in the three worlds. We can never repay you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, nor do we even want that day to ever come that we are not eternally indebted to you. Our prayer is that the full meaning and purpose of our human form of life, which you have causelessly unveiled to us, be fulfilled with Your Divine Grace.

Let us remain forever as your tiny servants, and Your Divine Grace our spotless and pure spiritual master. Keep us forever as dust under your lotus feet.

Your eternal servants at ISKCON Baltimore

## Berkeley

All Glories to Your Divine Grace

The struggle for existence does not take place for one in spiritual consciousness. You are absorbed in your service to Kṛṣṇa, and so you are unaffected by the three modes.

*daivī hy eṣā guṇa-mayī mama māyā duratyayā*  
*mām eva ye prapadyante māyām etāṁ taranti te*  
*(Bg 7.14)*

We are here in the material world, and except for yourself, most of us are in a material consciousness. By your mercy you have descended to this stool house, bringing with you the spiritual world and spiritual knowledge.

Just as Kṛṣṇa descends in the human form, you appear displaying Lord Caitanya's pastimes—but the unintelligent, such as myself, have no appreciation for pure devotees such as yourself.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *jagad-guru*, enacting this *Saṅkīrtana* mission right here in this most degraded culture. The people here are of the lowest caliber of *mlecchas* and *śūdras*. And you, my divine master, have left your beloved Goloka Vṛndāvana to come and engage them properly in Kṛṣṇa's service.

The sinful activities of these Western people are but a reflection of my own sinister and envious mentality. You have given me the process for cleansing my body and soul, but I become slack unless chastised often. I pray to you for the strength to follow your teachings.

For all the knowledge and happiness you are bringing to us, the only thing you ask in return is that we distribute your mercy to others in the form of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gitā*, which can make the whole world Kṛṣṇa conscious. We are so fortunate to have a chance to partake in this spiritual activity of dispersing knowledge which frees the soul of millions of sinful reactions.

However, most unfortunately, I have no strong desire to carry out your order. I simply want to live comfortably in the temple, enjoying my senses and doing whatever service satisfies me. By my self-motivated mentality, I have no compassion or love, and making someone else happy is only secondary.

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā*: not affected by these material modes in which I am so deeply entwined, just as a snake tightly wraps his victim who is so foolish as to venture into dangerous areas.

You have taught me that "Humility means that one should not be anxious to have the satisfaction of being honored by others." But when my service is praised I become enlivened, and when I am chastised I become morose.

You are unattached to everything in this world, and you are drawn to Kṛṣṇa like filings to a powerful magnet. You are always telling us about Kṛṣṇa and His pastimes and His service:

*satataṁ kīrtayanto māṁ yatantaś ca dṛḍha-vratāḥ*  
*namasyantaś ca māṁ bhaktyā nitya-yuktā upāsate*  
*(Bg 9.14)*

If only I can be attracted by your unparalleled good qualities and leave the undesirable association of my mind. I am struggling for material satisfaction, controlled by my unbridled senses and my monkeylike activities, which cover any of my good features.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have saved me from falling off the cliff of human life into the cycle of evolution. If somehow, by your mercy alone, I can keep on distributing your books, and develop some appreciation of your appearance, then my life will become all-auspicious and successful.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept my prostrate obeisances at your lotus feet, and please don't kick me away into the mire of nescience from which you dragged me.

I pray to you to give me the strength to carry out your order.

Your fallen and offensive servants,  
New Jagannatha Puri Dhama, Berkeley

## BBT Library Party

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our prostrate obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu*, Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī states:

*anāsaktasya viṣayān yathārham upayuñjataḥ*  
*nirbandhaḥ kṛṣṇa-sambandhe yuktaṁ vairāgyam ucyate*

**“When one is not attached to anything, but at the same time accepts everything in relation to Kṛṣṇa, one is rightly situated above possessiveness."**

As the most exalted *rūpānuga*, you embody this, Śrīla Prabhupāda. For example, if a wretched, bewildered *jīva* reluctantly offers some service to you, as Kṛṣṇa's most intimate devotee you display your magnanimous nature by accepting that service, however faulty, and the *jīva*benefits by helping spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Therefore, you are the most expert and wonderful spiritual master, and we thank you for engaging us on the BBT Library Party. Although you found us uneducated (even by *mleccha* standards), you have enthused and dispatched us to preach to the "intelligensia" who, at best, are no better than drunken Jagāis and Mādhāis in the garb of "Ph.D., professor, and dean." Now, you are enabling even them to become purified by serving Kṛṣṇa. Some are purchasing your books; some are recommending them to libraries. And some scholars, in circles where ass-like braying is the theme song, have suddenly produced sweet praises for you and your books, such as these:

"It is axiomatic that no book can be expected to entirely satisfy all its potential readers. Here is one, however, which can be said to come remarkably close to that ideal...." (re: *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*)

"It is really astonishing how Śrīla Prabhupāda is able to translate the entire *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* single handed, and when one comes to think of this, apart from his other great literary works, one is tempted to wonder if he is not the same Vyāsa Muni reborn today to adapt his own old work into a universal language of this age for the spiritual upliftment of the modern man."

Let them utilize your books more and more, as textbooks, reference books, required or extra-credit reading, and, eventually, as the solution to their ghostly-haunted lives of mental speculation and fruitive activity. Already, hundreds have written reviews; thousands have purchased individually; and at least two thousand libraries (public, private, and university libraries alike, representing eighteen countries on four continents) have utilized BBT's standing order service to acquire a complete set of your books for their patrons.

So let the philosophers and religionists irrigate their "comparative approach" with the life-giving nectar of your preaching, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Let the literary specialists satisfy their passion for adventure and love by reading *kṛṣṇa-līlā* as rendered through the lotuslike lips of a pure devotee. May the sanskritists and Indologists, squinting at the diacritics, expand their vision to the extent of actually understanding the books they study, by utilizing your editions. Even the sociologists, artists, lawyers, and biologists can utilize your books, for as you said, "I touch on these fields whenever possible."

Grasping for words to represent even one percent of your divine glory on this Vyāsa-pūjā day, we simply offer to you our Library Party services as one more example of what unqualified disciples can do with the help of a bona fide guru:

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*  
*(Cc. Madhya 17.80)*

**"By the mercy of the guru, even a dumb man can become the greatest orator, and even a lame man can cross mountains."**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please allow us to utilize our lives in your service under any circumstances.

Your eternal servants, The International BBT Library Party
