## Guatemala City

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**"I was born in the darkest ignorance and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my humble obeisances unto his lotus feet."**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

**"I offer my humble obeisances to my spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa because he has taken shelter at His lotus feet."**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy like our fortune is inconceivably unlimited. Really we have no qualification to be worthy of the dust of your lotus feet; neither do we have in our stock any truly pious activity. Therefore, it is just because of our inconceivable good luck (which is due, indeed, to the causeless mercy of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa), that our way was met with yours, and it is only because of your infinite causeless mercy that you kindly have picked us up from the filthy bog of sinful life; therefore, on this most auspicious day, we bow to your lotus feet and we implore you:

Please O our eternal father, O our lord and master birth after birth, don't allow that we become bewildered and captivated by the illusory energy (*māyā*) of the Lord, in such a way that we forget the constant service to your lotus feet. We do not dare even to ask you that you remove the reactions of our sinful activities; simply we are begging that you allow us to develop a strong attachment to the service of your divine lotus feet and the ability to remember our eternal indebtedness to you,

Let all the universe sing the glories of *patita-pāvana*, our beloved spiritual master, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Your fallen servants of Guatemala Temple:

Prabhu dāsa brahmacārī / Kalpataru dāsa brahmacārī / Arture Mazariegos Prabhu / Harināmānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Carlos

## Guyana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. This is our humble effort for Vyāsa-Pūjā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda BENEDICTS EVERYONE

Śrīla Prabhupāda! the sage among so-called human society.  
Teaches everyone to serve Rādhā Kṛṣṇa Deity.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! the only true saint on this planet,  
Draws everyone to serve Kṛṣṇa as iron is pulled by some magnet.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! the supreme revolutionist of the Absolute Truth,  
Awakens everyone sleeping in māyā with his lotuslike boot.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! the best example of a pure devotee,  
Reaches everyone beyond the Māyāvādīs' concept of becoming one with the brahma-jyotir.

Śrīla Prabhupāda the real mahātmājī among the fake mahātmās or durātmās,  
Explains to everyone the relationship between the ātmā and Paramātmā.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! Kṛṣṇa's most exalted servant at the present moment,  
Stops everyone from learning how to lament.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! the real defamer of the modern scientist, religionist,  
political activist, communist, democratist, the gross materialist, and the atheist,  
Tells everyone not to follow those miscreants that pose themselves as real educationalists.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! the foremost authority on Vedic culture,  
Gives everyone a chance to stop acting like a vulture.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! who can change the world from darkness to light,  
Helps everyone meditate on Kṛṣṇa all day and all night.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead,  
Allows everyone to return to Goloka Vṛndāvana to live forever instead.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! teaches us about Lord Caitanyadeva,  
Enthuses everyone to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda instructs us to take Kṛṣṇa-prasāda,  
Encourages everyone to become Kṛṣṇa consciously mad.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! travels from his country to others,  
Just to instruct everyone that the cow is one of our mothers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! turns caṇḍālas into Vaiṣṇavas,  
Defeats everyone, stops them from becoming big, big asuras.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! the bona fide spiritual master,  
Heals everyone that is covered by material nature's plaster.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda! the founder ācārya,  
Takes everyone out of the bondage of māyā.

Your servants,

## Honk Kong

*nala-veṇu-śaras-tanba-kuśa-kīcaka-gahvaram*  
*eka evātiyāto 'ham adrākṣaṁ vipinaṁ mahat*  
*ghoraṁ pratibhayākāraṁ vyālolūka-śivājiram*

**"I then passed alone through many forests of bamboo, sharp grass, weeds and caves, which were very difficult to go through alone. I visited deep, dark and dangerously fearful forests, which were the play yards of snakes, owls and jackals." (*****Bhāg.*** **1.6.13)**

The duty of the *parivrājakācārya* (mendicant) is to travel everywhere and enlighten the inhabitants of the earth with the message of God. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you took the order of your spiritual master as your life and soul, left your blissful *bhajana-kuṭira* (place of prayer) in Vṛndāvana, the serene land of Lord Kṛṣṇa's birth and pastimes, and at an age when ordinary men do not venture forth from their sitting rooms and front porches, came alone to the deep, dark and dangerously fearful concrete forests of the naked city.

O swanlike one, since that blessed beginning in New York you have been miraculously snatching countless conditioned souls from jackallike lives, dousing the raging forest fire of material existence with showers of your mercy and nectarous wisdom. Unto such a spiritual master I offer my obeisances again and again and again and again.

On this holy occasion of the anniversary of your appearance, our prayer is to be blessed with one dram from your fountain of courage, one drop from your sea of conviction, one speck of dust from your ever-traveling lotus feet. May we not fear the weeds and caves, strange and dangerous places, but push on and on, following in your unfailing footsteps, to inundate with love of Kṛṣṇa, every town and village.

Lomaśa Ṛṣi dāsa / Yaśomatī-suta dāsa adhikarī / Nitya-līlā Sakhi-devī dāsī / Bhakta Gulab

## Honolulu

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our obeisances and prayers of gratitude at your lotus feet.

*tvaṁ sad-gurur naḥ paramaṁ ca daivataṁ*  
*yasyānuvṛttyā kṛtino babhūvima*

**"You are our spiritual master and supreme worshipable deity. By following in your footsteps we have become successful in every respect."** ***(Bhāg*****. 1.11.7)**

Certainly now that you have taken us on as your disciples, we must be the most fortunate few persons on this earth, which lies so helplessly besieged by the awesome forces of the age. If we can just hold fast to your mercy, you have assured us we will always remain without a doubt saved. Please, then, tell us again day after day, reminding us as we need to be reminded, of the simple formula Kṛṣṇa has given: *man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru \[*Bg. 18.65\]. As you have explained, Kṛṣṇa is asking only four things. "Always think of Me," by being always engaged in hearing and chanting.

"Become My devotee," by accepting the association of devotees and maintaining without fail the basic principles. "Worship Me," both as the Deity in the temple by regular service and as the Lord of sacrifice by striving to spread His glories all over the world. And "Offer your homage unto Me," continuing to acknowledge Kṛṣṇa's kindness even within the most trying difficulty.

Perhaps you are even a little satisfied with our most weakhearted efforts to serve. Please, therefore, continue to rain your undeserved blessings on us. Defeat all our unnecessary diversions and set us straight more and more on this supreme perfectional path.

Your humble servants of lSKCON Hawaii.

## Houston

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*ceto-darpaṇa-mārjanaṁ bhava-mahā-dāvāgni-nirvāpaṇaṁ*  
*śreyaḥ-kairava-candrikā-vitaraṇaṁ vidyā-vadhū-jīvanam*  
*ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaṁ prati-padaṁ pūrṇāmṛtāsvādanaṁ*  
*sarvātma-snapanaṁ paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam*  
*Cc. Antya 20.12*

**"All glories to the Śrī Kṛṣṇa** ***Saṅkīrtana*****, which cleanses the heart of all the dust accumulated for years together and extinguishes the fire of conditional life, of repeated birth and death. This** ***Saṅkīrtana*** **movement is the prime benediction for humanity at large because it spreads the rays of the benediction moon. It is the life of all transcendental knowledge. It increases the ocean of transcendental bliss, and it enables us to fully taste the nectar for which we are always anxious."**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our respectful and humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Your transcendental appearance is a display of Śrī Kṛṣṇa's grace upon all conditioned souls. He has sent you to mercifully distribute the *bhakti-latā*, the creeper of devotional service. Without this creeper of devotional service all living entities are bound to transmigrate from one species to another and from one planet to another, never finding real happiness. You have given us all the opportunity to return home, back to Godhead.

Such a matchless gift cannot be repaid. However, we must enthusiastically attempt to repay you even though we may always be unable. Our repayment is to follow exactly as you have directed, chant sixteen *good* rounds, follow the four regulative principles and preach this message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We understand that to please you is the perfection of our lives. Our fervent prayer to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is that you give us the ability to recollect that we are your eternal servants and meant to engage all of our senses in your service.

Your servants, Houston Yatra

## Kṛṣṇa-Balarama TSKP

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dearest Father,*

Please accept our most fallen and worthless obeisances at your sacred lotus feet. We are certainly incapable of saying anything poetic, and any attempt to praise you will be marred by our filthy consciousness, our words remain the mumbling of fools.

We are facing the burning of *saṁsāra*. *Māyā* is dancing before our senses and you and Kṛṣṇa are calling us to come home. We are running towards your shelter. Father, but we are crippled, and we are always falling down and getting lost on the path you tell us to follow. Dark clouds of illusion are surrounding us and death like a fierce dog is closing in on our so-called youth. O Father! We are terrified of this material world. It is the worst nightmare; we are crying. Lost in the forest of illusion we fall again and again, but there is no time to count our bruises and lament. We are running toward you begging for shelter; we are running with you in defeating the atheism of the world, spreading the brilliance of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and we are running for you in the performance of our prescribed *dharmas* trying to please you—our most wonderful and divine *guru-mahārāja*.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! We are your servants, souls surrendered unto you, now and forever. Kindly tell us what to do. We remain,

*A group of fools,*

Manu Thākura dāsa brahmacārī / Girivanadhāri dāsa brahmacārī / Tribūvanātha dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma dāsa brahmacārī / Viṣṇu Datta dāsa brahmacārī

## Laguna Beach

Unless one is recognized by the Supreme Personality of Godhead by one's devotional service, the human form of life is wasted. A devotee must therefore know how to render service unto Kṛṣṇa and thus be recognized by Him. It is explained in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* that one should serve both the spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa simultaneously to progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Service rendered to Kṛṣṇa under the direction of the spiritual master is bona fide service because the spiritual master is the manifested representative of Kṛṣṇa.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari \[Kṛṣṇa\]."**

Therefore, when one satisfies the spiritual master he satisfies Kṛṣṇa.

O beloved Gurudeva, by your mercy we can now understand that everyone on this planet is enamored by the illusory satisfaction of sense gratification. Being fully engaged in illusory activities they are reluctant to take to the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement because they are unaware of the benefits of surrendering to Kṛṣṇa and so they are subjected to various miserable conditions of material existence. They are foolishly attached to so-called society, friendship and love, which simply produce different kinds of miserable conditions. Everyone, both man and woman, is attached to this material existence and is engaged in cheating one another in a great society of the cheaters and the cheated.

Modern society encourages people to become careless about spiritual realization, so everyone is full of anxieties thinking, "What is to be done? What is next?" And because they are strongly bound by material desires they continue to remain in craziness.

It is indeed very difficult to get out of the material entanglement but somehow or other we were favored by Kṛṣṇa. By His mercy only we were given the opportunity to associate with a pure devotee like Your Divine Grace.

We can therefore conclude that we must be specifically favored by Kṛṣṇa, otherwise how is it we are becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and are engaging in the preaching work of this sublime Vaiṣṇava philosophy?

Your unworthy servants, the community of New Jhārikhaṇḍa

## Lima

To remember  
Not autumn springs,  
Wonderful waterfalls gleam  
Nor sunset beams

But  
to remember  
You  
Savior of our heart  
Graceful knight  
Whose words have broken  
With sādhu sword  
The nightlikefog of  
Māyā's dark

To remember

You  
And not forget again  
We pray.

## London (Bhaktivedanta Manor)

**Gurudeva**

1\. No modernistic soup you brew;  
Your precepts taught of yore:  
Eternal tenets, tested true,  
Confirmed by Vedic law.

2\. A stirring Gospel, time-intact,  
Awaking dormant ways:  
"Twixt loved and lover sleeps a pact  
Inherent for all days."

3\. Corruptive cheats, who strangle truth,  
Have crippled mosque and church;  
But Truth has held Himself aloof,  
Avoiding vain research.

4\. The truth is but for those sincere  
Who turn their hearts to you,  
evealed to those of earnest ear;  
But they are far and few.

5\. Although the truth resounds aloud,  
Throughout the globe declared,  
The brainless booby, puffed too proud,  
From death is never spared.

6\. O woe to him who turns you down  
And disregards your word:  
He's lost, alas, a luckless clown,  
By lust alone bestirred.

7\. The simple truth is pure and plain:  
Śrī Kṛṣṇa is our Lord.  
One needs but chant His holy name,  
His mercy down is poured.

8\. We're not these skin-and-boney bags,  
But are the souls within;  
Yet, brandishing false fleshy flags,  
We flap in squalid sin.

9\. Repent your wicked ways, O man!  
Surrender at his feet!  
Remold your life to suit his plan,  
And progress you will meet.

10\. Don shining hope! Doff dull despair!  
Bear japa beads in hand!  
Ascend with care the narrow stair  
To greet the promised land!

11\. Be led by him along the way  
That saints and sages trod!  
Enjoy the gradual dawn of day,  
O brilliant son of God!

12\. Attract those trapped in groggy world  
To leap along behind!  
Triumphant banners fly unfurled.  
True life and freedom find!

13\. Pay homage unto him, all men!  
Uphold his noble cause!  
His Grace Divine will 'light on them  
Who strive to keep his laws.

14\. Although I'm chanting Kṛṣṇa's name,  
I cannot gain a taste.  
O master, bless me with a grain,  
Lest human life I waste.

15\. Redeem me from this wave-tossed world,  
Where I am but a slave,  
By surging sensual urges hurled.  
O save me, Gurudeva.

*Our dearmost worshipable, most glorious Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble *daṇḍavats*at your holy lotus feet. We are your most fallen disciples. We have no intelligence to understand the full import of your desires, or your greatness-still, by your grace, we wish to glorify you.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

You are the most intimate associate of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and yet you have so kindly come here. You left the holy *dhāma* of Vṛndāvana to associate with us, who are too low even to be classified amongst the untouchables in the *varṇāśrama* system.

You have taught us the missing secret of life, which is practically unknown in this demoniac culture in which we have taken birth.

*Ahaṁ brahmāsmi*-I am not this body, I am spirit soul. What is the business of the spirit soul? *Jivera 'svarūpa' haya, kṛṣnera 'nitya-*dāsa'- our eternal occupation is to engage in the transcendental loving service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa.

*sei sarva-vedera 'abhidheya' nāma*  
*sādhana-bhakti haite haya premera udgama*

**"By practicing this regulated devotional service under the direction of the spiritual master, certainly one awakens his dormant love of God. This is called** ***abhidheya*****," (*****Cc. Ādi-līlā*** **7.142)**

You have so mercifully taught us the most confidential of all knowledge, the activity of the eternal spirit soul in the spiritual world.

Now we are actually coming to life, by your grace. Before we met with your mercy, we were engaged in so many activities, all more or less entangling, and mostly degrading and impious. Now we can understand that, although we had such a high opinion of ourselves, although we were so firmly convinced of our positions as lords of material nature, actually we were not even exhibiting symptoms of life.

*jīvañ chavo bhāgavatāṅghri-reṇuṁ*  
*na jātu martyo 'bhilabheta yas tu*  
*śrī-viṣṇu-padyā manujas tulasyāḥ*  
*śvasañ chavo yas tu na veda gandham*

**"The person who has not at any time received the dust of the feet of a pure devotee of the Lord upon his head certainly is a dead body. And the person who has never experienced the flavor of the** ***tulasī*** **leaves from the lotus feet of the Lord is also a dead body, although breathing." (*****Bhāg*** **2.3.23).**

Real life does not exist outside the approved path of devotional service, beginning with *śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣnoḥ*. We are experiencing that we are actually becoming enlivened by this process of devotional service. Without devotional service, under your instructions, our breathing is no better than the pulsing of bellows. Actually, our every living gesture and activity is simply by your mercy. We are made of your mercy. *Tomāra karunā-sāra.*

Anything performed outside the scope of your instructions is simply conditioned activity under the influence of the three modes of material nature.

Your dearly beloved Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who can attract millions of Cupids, who is enlivening the cows and the senses, and who is the Lord of the *gopīs*, is known as Bhagavān, the possessor of all opulence. We can clearly see that you are also exhibiting these opulences by His grace. Who else is so wealthy that he owns large properties in major cities all over the world, as well as farms, gorgeous *mandiras* and prosperous businesses? Who else is so beautiful that people all over the world will weep and dance in ecstasy to see him? Who else is so influential that he can purify the polluted atmosphere of Kali, which destroys all the good qualities of the human beings? Who is so all-knowing that he can determine what is right action in every sphere of human activity? Who is so powerful that he can defeat the greatest philosophers, politicians, and religionists? And who is so renounced that he can live surrounded by such opulence, and still sleep two or four hours a day, and dedicate his life and soul to the ultimate welfare of all? Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so famous that at every moment, as the sun takes his course, somewhere on the planet someone is bowing and reciting prayers in your praise. Your appearance is epoch-making, and your characteristics and mission are described in the Vedic scriptures. *Vede gāy yāhāra carito*.

We regret that our devotion and love for you is hardly more than skin-deep, hardly more than sentiment. You have said that when you write your translations and purports, you are thinking that this is to be a guide for the next ten thousand years. On the other hand, we find it difficult to maintain enthusiasm for more than forty-eight hours the continuous *kirtana* which you requested.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are like children. You are actually protecting us and nourishing us with the milk of the Vedic wisdom. We are as helplessly dependent on you as a naked baby is dependent on the mother. You are our real parent and guardian, for your protection is infallible.

You have written that this chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare is like the genuine cry of the baby for the mother. You have awakened us to our desperate plight and taught us to cry.

This process of devotional service is so sublime, so simple, so direct and so profound. We have only to abide strictly by your instructions, to have full faith in the chanting of the holy name. We humbly pray that you will continue to shower us with your mercy, so that we can make our lives successful, by your grace.

*Your most fallen servants,*

## London (Bury Place)

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya*  
*jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena*  
*tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

You chant Kṛṣṇa 's glories to spread His fame  
to cleanse our hearts and make us sane  
Endeavouring, determined, you never relent  
You know the Lord, it's by Him you've been sent.

Constantly engaged in serving the Lord  
Your love can never from Him be called  
In this whole creation there is no space  
for you there's nothing but His lotus face.

Resolute in purpose, your aim is one  
to make us all happy, you 're never done  
You're the self-realized soul who never sits still  
with no personal desire, but the Lord's to fulfill.

Śrī Bhagavad-gītā is proof of its own  
like the sun with no shadow you've vividly shown  
You are the proof, O Prabhupada, lord of my sight  
I challenge the man who can match your might!

Analyze carefully and make your test  
Where is the solution to disease and death?  
Just apply your logic to my guru  
His mercy you'll find gives eternal youth.

Devoted to Kṛṣṇa, you never lament  
You joyfully work. "It's for service we're meant!"  
Person bhāgavata, there's no fault in you  
You demonstrate this knowledge and show us it's true.

To write about you is to become purified  
Remember your feet and in you confide  
This day of yours is your mercy as well  
Boundless your love, you've saved us from hell.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

## Los Angeles

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

To properly glorify your qualities and pastimes is as difficult as glorifying Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is unlimited and unpredictable, and being fully surrendered to Him, you have the same features. There is no way we can ever repay you, either now or at any time in the future. You have given us everything; we have given you nothing but trouble.

In Māyāpur, you have built a transcendental city to cool our hearts and minds and give us the peace which escapes all living entities in the creation. While we walked happily to the Gaṅga, you lay sick in your room, bearing the sinful reactions of millions of sinful lives of your disciples. The next day, you initiated over two hundred more. Who can possibly begin to understand your selflessness?

In Jagannātha Purī, some *paṇḍitas* chanted beautiful prayers for you in glorification of Lord Jagannātha. With tears in your eyes, you told them, "I only want that these American boys can see Lord Jagannātha." You never stop thinking of our welfare. We have done nothing to deserve your mercy. It is causeless mercy.

Despite all difficulties you never give up preaching about Kṛṣṇa. One night in Māyāpur, despite a crippling illness, you preached to us about determination in devotional service. You cited the example of Jaṭāyu, the vulture who was killed while trying to stop Rāvaṇa from kidnapping Sītā. You explained that although Jaṭāyu was unable to save Sītā, this does not mean that he was unsuccessful. Because he endeavored to the best of his ability to serve Lord Rāmacandra, he was successful, even though he failed to achieve the result. You are thus teaching us to follow your example of ceaselessly fighting for Kṛṣṇa, ignoring all obstacles.

Your doctor came to see you due to the severity of your illness. His purpose was to order you not to speak, so you could regain your energy. When he arrived in your room, you immediately took the opportunity to preach to him about Kṛṣṇa consciousness. *No force within this material world is powerful enough to stop your preaching.*

As we reluctantly left your association in Māyāpur to go to Vṛndāvana, we heard another amazing example of your tenacity. Your illness was so severe that you were unable to move. You commented that your father had died of the selfsame disease. We were all terrified, and we didn't know what to do. Then we heard your final comment. You started making plans for a preaching tour. We couldn't believe it. Later on in Vṛndāvana, one devotee was standing on the roof of the guest house, watching you as you sat in great pain on the roof of your house. To his amazement, you spent one full hour attempting to lift up your microphone. You then proceeded to translate the Tenth Canto. On another occasion you stated that you will go on preaching until your last breath.

You have given up everything to make the world Krsna conscious. You alone are powerful enough to manifest the will of Caitanya Mahāprabhu in this world.

We can only lie in the dust of your feet and pray for your mercy. Please give us the ability to follow your instructions. Please empower us to act in such a way as to help you in your service, rather than hinder you. Your mercy is already manifested unlimitedly. Please empower us to accept it.

Your disciples in New Dvārakā

**Resident Sanyāsīs**

## Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To the unbinding one, free from the taxes of this law-made world, to the swan-like mendicant who begs and gives divine beverage of love of God. Unto him, we salute unto him, we bow down in respect, as the reed bends in the wind.

O messenger of the truth, your fame bounds no limits! Like a soldier, you go to fight this world, and knowledge is spread everywhere by your strength. Your weapon is mercy and your gift is causeless. You are the cause of mercy, for you are the proof of God. Kṛṣṇa is your slogan, Kṛṣṇa is everything, with that faith you have knocked at the door of our heart. "Wake up, wake up! Kṛṣṇa is waiting" ... but we say "happy" is this world, and of death we laugh. Irony is our game and agony the result.

You came in this world to save us all, master, but ungrateful we are; we do not respect your kindness. We like to glorify the greatness of your deeds, but we are lazy and blind. We like to pray for grace for us, to be guided on the way.

O Prabhupāda, you are our lord and master, the very life of our soul; we are children bereft of all knowledge, why unto us, that love has been given? How wonderful, master, that by grace we have been chosen. O master, king of our heart, please tell us, who are you, master? We are degraded and low but still proud of ourselves, please let us grasp your lotus feet. Praying for inspiration, we like to respect you, O lord, O king of this world, master of all, can we become your servants?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi-līlā* 1.45) it is said:

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees”.**

When will we be able to realize that you actually are non-different from Kṛṣṇa? When will we be able to appreciate what you have given us? When will we be able to fulfill your desires? When will we stop committing offenses at your lotus-like feet?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, these are questions which we have been asking ourselves so many times. Especially they come to our miserable minds whenever you have to suffer from our offenses, like early this year in Māyāpur. At such moments we feel ourselves the most wretched persons in the creation, knowing full well that our offensive, neglectful and selfish service is causing you suffering. You have come like a loving father to deliver and save the lost children, but we, your eternal sons and daughters, pay only little attention to your instructions of how to come back home. We are so puffed up that sometimes we even make up our own plans to save ourselves. But like the frog in the well, we'll simply be eaten up by the snake.

We remember one day at the Ganges in Māyāpur, your secretary was reading notes from a diary. Someone was asking you: "Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, be merciful unto me!" Immediately you replied: "You be merciful unto me."

You have given your mercy freely to everyone in innumerable ways—actually you are mercy personified—there is no need to keep asking for it. But have you ever received our "mercy"? The moment we can understand that we also have to be merciful towards you—by following your orders, by preaching purely, by doing our duties without selfish motivation—at that time we'll be of some assistance in your mission of changing the demoniac face of the world.

Until that moment, however, we will have to simply pray to be able to remain fixed in the dust of your lotus feet. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, don't kick us away! There is no meaning to life without you! You are the only hope for us! Without you there is no chance of becoming pure. Therefore, we pray that some day we may understand your exalted position as the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead and be able to act accordingly, and in this way our offenses might stop and this may let you complete your mission to benedict every living being with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. May this be our desire on this most auspicious day of the year.

Your worthless disciples, ISKCON Mauritius center

Kapila dāsa / Śaṅkāśa dāsa / Śrī-niketana dāsa

## Māyāpur

All glories unto you dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
On Vyāsa-pūjā-this holy of holy days!  
A messenger descended from the Lord's abode,  
Dispersing ignorance's darkness like sun's rays.

Weaponless, yet armed with guru's order,  
Dear Prabhupāda has crossed the raging sea.  
No one imagined what he would bring-  
From birth and death his mercy sets us free.

Falsely proud due to their scientific gropings  
Scientists fool people with hope upon false hope,  
Not even knowing the goal for which we're living.  
Prabhupāda's true science gives real light and hope!

Great Nārada Muni, cursed to be ever on the move;  
Likewise Prabhupāda remains his true descendant.  
With hundred palatial temples to stay around the world  
He's always reaching, touring—nowhere a resident.

Mother forces son to go to school each day.  
Mother's "force" is welfare lest son's life go astray.  
Childlike India imitates void Western culture,  
Prabhupāda cries and battles to preserve the Vedic way.

Gauḍīya Maṭha has split and shattered.  
Losing love and trust's fate we all can see.  
Let's unitedly serve dear Prabhupāda!  
No halting ISKCON's march-except internally!

Śrīla Prabhupāda struggling hard eleven years  
That there may be "American Kṛṣṇa's".  
Now "Hare Kṛṣṇa Bona fide," the Judge proclaimed.  
Now our turn to fulfill Prabhupāda's wishes.

Mrdanga 's "big & small," Prabhupāda plays them all!  
BOOKS—55 million printed—a miracle!  
"Now double this year's sale!"—let all learn Kṛṣṇa's tale.  
With Prabhupāda we'll overcome all obstacles!

Lord Gaurāṅga's message will save the world!  
In "every town and village" His name will be sung!  
The prediction must come true—It is His will!  
And Prabhupāda's the one founder let it be rung!

Now at Māyāpur—the center of all that be  
Trying humbly to fulfill the ācārya's dream.  
May the entire world all know and love  
Lord Caitanya, who's giving freely Kṛṣṇa-prema.

Prabhupāda's our life and soul!  
His lotus feet the refuge of mind and heart.  
Fixing his service as our only goal-  
We pray that from his order we'll not depart.

**ALL GLORIES TO**  
**HIS DIVINE GRACE ŚRĪ ŚRĪMAD**  
**A.C. BHAKTIVEDANTA SWAMI PRABHUPĀDA**  
**PARIVRĀJAKĀCĀRYA-PARAMAHAṀSA,**  
**OUR BELOVED SPIRITUAL MASTER!**

## Melbourne

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. Your position of *ācāryadeva*is to be constantly glorified, because you are the direct representative and most intimate confidential servitor of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Radha-Kṛṣṇa. It is stated in *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*:

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

By accepting the instructions of such a spiritual master and applying them with unflinching faith, one is assured of crossing this dangerous ocean of material nescience and becoming firmly situated in the eternal sphere of spiritual varieties that constitute the *jīva*soul's *sanātana dhārma*. One cannot possibly hope for advancement on this path without the guidance and blessings of the pure devotee of the Lord. *Guru Kṛṣṇa prasāde paya bhakti-latā-bīja \[Cc.* Madhya 19.151\]. The combined mercy of both guru and Kṛṣṇa is required to be situated safely beyond reach of the influence of the *māyā* potency. *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (11.3.21) implores:

*tasmād guruṁ prapadyeta jijñāsuḥ śreya uttamam*  
*śābde pare ca niṣṇātaṁ brahmaṇy upaśamāśrayam*

**"Any person who seriously desires to achieve real happiness must seek out a bona fide spiritual master and take shelter of him by initiation. The qualification of his spiritual master is that he must have realized the conclusions of the scriptures by deliberation and must be able to convince others of these conclusions."**

Through your books, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are revealing to this estranged world a vast wealth of Vedic knowledge which can actually benefit the conditioned soul and ease him out of his deplorable material predicament. To this end, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have devoted your transcendental energies—your mercy shines like the new sunrise dissipating the darkness. In the *Vāyu Purāṇa* an *ācārya* is defined as one who knows the import of Vedic literatures, explains the purpose of the *Vedas*, abides by their rules and regulations and teaches his disciples to act in the same way.

We are not praying for you to shower your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda—that has already been done by your compassionate grace, for you are *jagad-guru*. Rather, we beg for the strength, determination and enthusiasm to gather this mercy from our lowly positions and to assist you in the glorious mission of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya *nāma*-s*aṅkīrtana* as you benedict the entire world.

We beg to remain eternally at your lotus feet, with the dust of all Vaiṣṇavas on our heads.

Your servants at Śrī Melbourne Mahāprabhu Mandira

## Mexico City

To His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, the protector of the devotees and dearest servant of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to you, O spiritual master, by whom we are relieved of the material suffering. Such suffering is so painful to the heart and difficult to surpass that it is impossible without your kind help. Therefore, in this, your yearly homage, we are making an offering unto you—our heart, our intelligence, and our endeavors—we offer everything unto you. We have accepted you as our lord and, therefore, everything belongs to you.

People who are not able to see your work in this world are too much covered by enviousness and their knowledge has no value. However, as the eternal time elapses, more and more persons will accept your certain and impressive victory in giving Kṛṣṇa consciousness to this insane and frantic, empty society.

Let all people hear your glories, your name, of your unlimited mercy. Let them know of your faultless character, of your spotless fame. Let them chant the holy names Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Rāma, and thus become Kṛṣṇa conscious, sane and honest persons, who accept this *bhagavat-prasāda*.

Please Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us to make this endeavor: to distribute your books and attain your mercy. Allow us to serve you birth after birth and take shelter in the sanctity of the dust of your lotus feet.

*Your fallen disciples of MEXICO YATRA*

## Mexico TSKP

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him."**

My dear father and spiritual master, please let me offer my most humble and insignificant obeisances unto your merciful lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the only sure shelter for a fallen soul. The causeless mercy of Śrī Śrī Radha-Kṛṣṇa is manifested in this ghastly material world through your munificent personality, by whom all conditioned souls are given, without discrimination, the chance to be free of the clutches of the material existence by chanting the holy names of the Lord: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

By chanting the holy names of the Lord under your protection, O pure devotee of the Lord, the most fallen among the fallen ones can qualify himself to realize the transcendental pastimes of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa

*janma karma ca me divyam evaṁ yo vetti tattvataḥ*  
*tyaktvā dehaṁ punar janma naiti mām eti so 'rjuna*

**"One who knows the transcendental nature of My appearance and activities does not, upon leaving the body, take his birth again in this material world, but attains My eternal abode, O Arjuna." (Bg. 4.9)**

O my dear master, I am always lonely and bewildered, walking on the abyss-brink. Kindly be merciful unto me and don't let me throw myself headlong into it. Make me your eternal servant.

YAJṢYNO VAI VIŚṆU TSKP MEXICO

Viraha Prakāśa Svāmī / Kṣiti Mohana dāsa brahmacārī / Advayajñāna dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Daniel

## Miami

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Your fathomless glories are so unlimited that in all of our attempts to appreciate them, we simply feel like frogs sitting in a well trying to appreciate the vastness of the unlimited ocean.

Since time immemorial we have been suffering within this material world, revolving on the unending wheel of birth and death. Driven by our insatiable and perverted desires, we have never even begun to find the first trace of relief from our continued misery. Now, by your inconceivable mercy, you have appeared to free us from this holocaust and deliver us back to home, back to Godhead. Your message of loving devotion is so simple and clear, but somehow or other, we are still foolishly attempting to hold on to this alien world and resist your divine instructions. Still, by your grace you are so expertly disentangling us who are so wretched and insincere and you are accepting our most fallen service. Undoubtedly you are the greatest servant of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu as all of your desires and actions have proven. You are completely devoid of any personal aspiration, and by knowing the will of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu you have completely absorbed yourself in that mood of unlimited compassion upon all of the fallen souls.

We can now only pray to you for your continued association, because without it we would remain eternally illusioned in this prison house of material existence. May we pray to you eternally for your continued mercy and the shade of your lotus feet.

New Naimiṣāraṇya Forest Devotee Roster

## Minneapolis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

A short while ago you came to America with one desire: to try to spread the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement of Lord Caitanya in the Western world. Empowered by the divine instructions of your *guru-mahārāja*, Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, you left a nice calm residence in Vṛndāvana to cross the dangerous oceans and humbly approach the people of New York to take up the Hare Kṛṣṇa chanting process. Now twelve years later you have so many disciples and temples not only in America but all around the world. This is indeed wonderful proof of your purity and spiritual strength.

We are continuing in the service of Lord Kṛṣṇa under your guidance, knowing well that by your authority this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement will go to every town and village around the world, and it will be the only cure for this materially infected society. Although we may fall and stumble in our attempts to help you, like a young child helping his father, please forgive us for our clumsiness and continue showering your mercy upon us, for that mercy is like nectar and tastes very sweet even to the lame.

We thank you for the opportunity to worship your appearance, for this will be a landmark in history as the appearance of the savior of the world. We continually worship your lotus feet which are the only way by which one can obtain pure devotional service. These lotus feet are glorified in all the three worlds and are worshipable by great personalities like Lord Brahmā. Indeed the soothing shelter of your lotus feet is the path of topmost perfection to the spiritual abode of Goloka Vṛndāvana.

Only by the mercy of the greatest *avatāra,* Lord Caitanya, could it have been possible to obtain the association of the greatest *ācārya*. Please be merciful unto us although we are offensive fools. Please keep your lotus feet always manifest in the core of our hearts.

Minneapolis Yatra

## Mombasa, Nairobi

1\. Śrīla Prabhupāda your words are all  
Steering us from māyā lest we fall,  
Our lives are meant for serving you  
Through thick and thin we'll still serve you,  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda the savior for all.

2\. The only strength that we can have  
Depends on the attention we pay and have,  
If we neglect your words of advice  
We shall fall to sin and vice,  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will now behave.

3\. The nectar flowing from your lotus feet  
Is cool and soothing like honey sweet,  
Our duty is to hand this nectar out  
Freeing everyone from all their doubts,  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda we'll serve your feet.

4\. We pray that Kṛṣṇa will give us  
The sound intelligence to remind us,  
That only by your glance  
Can we break loose and dance,  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, your words we trust.

5\. Your books reveal the truth without doubt  
Revealing the demons with a strong clout,  
Your books are our friends  
For they'll save us in the end.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda destroyer of doubt.

6\. You are preaching here in this dark place  
To a most demoniac and uncivilized race,  
You are the greatest preacher of all  
For your mercy reaches here and stops the fall,  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda you are king of all race.

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you, O spiritual master, because you are so patiently spoon-feeding us who are like envious adolescents, the nectar from the unfathomable ocean of the mercy of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. When will we stop spitting out good fortune for the germs of material activity and instead surrender to "the ecstasy of intoxication by excessive drinking of the strong and tasty wine of the love of Lord Hari."

"My lord, devotees like your good self are verily holy places personified. Because you carry the Personality of Godhead within your heart, you turn all places into places of pilgrimage." (*Bhāg*. 1.13.10) The Supreme Lord is not only within your heart, most blessed Śrīla Prabhupāda, but as it is stated in *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Ādi*. 1.61), you are identical with the Lord because you are constantly engaged in His service.

Let me take shelter of those devotees who are most expert at pleasing you by their constant service and let me beg from them a special favor—to be able to write something suitable to offer unto your lotus feet.

**Prabhu-Datta Deśa**

(Place of residence given by the Lord) *Cc. Antya* 4.144

My heart's not in it anymore.  
I'll not approach even one more door.  
These cars are filled with lusty whores.  
Strong desires which number crores.

It's just beginning to get cold,  
The wind is biting, the motel room's old.  
These men are new, they can't be told.  
I'm quite ashamed for books not sold.

I'm leading these men but I need a guide,  
I need a shelter, I want to hide.  
Please help me Prabhupada, don't let me slide.  
Where to turn with your consciousness fried?

Phone to Memphis. Dhṛṣṭadyumna's there,  
he'll help me find a shelter here.  
He read Eighth Canto across that wire  
And began my path to the devotional fire.

"I'm falling," I said. "A wife," he suggested.  
"Mahārāja!" I remarked, a little disgusted.  
"A leader of men must be fixed up.  
If not, your orange robes, you'll have to give up."

Maybe he's right, I thought walking away,  
Checking for coins and a little dismayed.  
How can I make it, at least let me try.  
O Prabhupāda, can this be the only reply?

Your answer came later in the form of a change  
Tamāla Kṛṣṇa suggested leaving 'home on the range.'  
"Start packing today you're Africa bound."  
Now I know without doubting his decision was sound.

The example was set by Śrī Rūpa and Sanātana,  
Leaving home to preach in Vṛndāvana.  
That land was their home for the work of the Lord.  
To follow them's done by these orders of yours:

Preach anywhere and everywhere this message sublime,  
Chant 'Hare Kṛṣṇa' and fall in Caitanya's line.  
Distribute the name anyway anytime  
They'll benefit even by one single line.

Give them prasāda and teach them to play.  
Dancing and chanting and some even stay  
To work and chant more. Please come every day.  
When working for Kṛṣṇa there's unlimited pay.

The men who stay here now chant sixteen rounds.  
With their progress increasing by leaps and bounds,  
They're tasting the nectar, and where is it found?  
In your books and prasāda and that tumultuous sound.

## Montreal

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dear Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories to your lotus feet. On this holy day of your most auspicious appearance, we fall in the dust at your lotus feet and beg for the ability to properly glorify your divine and magnanimous character. You are the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead, freely bestowing your causeless mercy upon the unqualified and ungrateful conditioned souls of Kali-yuga. By your grace the entire material universe is awakening from an age-long sleep of godless ignorance to the joyous and eternal sound of Lord Caitanya's *harer nāma-Saṅkīrtana*. We are amongst the most fallen of living entities and yet, somehow or other, by your causeless mercy, you have glanced in our direction, and now our lives have become successful. Who can properly describe the glories of your causeless mercy?

In the Eleventh Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is stated by the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself:

"My dear Uddhava, the spiritual master must be accepted not only as my representative, but as My very self. He must never be considered on the same level with an ordinary human being. One should never be envious of the spiritual master, as one may be envious of an ordinary man. The spiritual master should always be seen as the representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and by serving the spiritual master, one is able to serve all the demigods."

Who but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, would deliver such low-born *mlecchas* and *yavanas* as ourselves, and turn us into Vaiṣṇavas, devotees of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa?

It is by your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we are tasting the nectar of devotional service to Śrī Govinda, the sweet transcendental cowherd boy of Vṛndāvana.

Byyour grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, every day we are bathing and dressing the Supreme Personality of Godhead in His *arcā-vigraha* form, which is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa Himself.

By your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are travelling to the holy places such as Māyāpur, Vṛndāvana, and Mathurā, although we have no qualifications. And by your grace we are visiting Śrī Rādha-Kuṇḍa, whose glories are unlimited, and which is unobtainable even by great sages such as Nārada and Sanaka. When has the world seen such mercy as yours?

Byyour grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are giving up our grossly sinful activities such as meat-eating, intoxication, gambling and illicit sex life, and instead we are learning to practice the rules and regulations of *vaidhi-bhakti*, under your expert guidance.

Byyour grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are beginning to understand who we are, and we are also beginning to help others understand who they are.

Byyour grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are beginning to understand who is God and who is not God. And by your grace we are praying to Him, instead of defaming Him.

By your grace alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are all going back home, back to Godhead. But most of all, by your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us your association and instructions. Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your grace, please bless us with the desire and ability to give these priceless gifts to each and every single conditioned soul in the material world.

All glories to your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda. May you please enjoy lasting good health.

With love from all the devotees of ISKCON Montreal

## New Delhi

O Prabhupāda  
You have come with grace divine  
Us lost, forgotten souls to find.  
Giving love, warm and tender  
Showing the way to sweet surrender.

O Prabhupāda  
Kicked hard by māyā, puffed-up and proud  
This thought of surrender we never allowed.  
Joyful debtors we will always be  
Your menial servants eternally.

O Prabhupāda  
You have given us life, you have given us hope  
Completely helpless, we could never cope  
With odds so great, a course so long.  
Only your grace can make us strong.

O Prabhupāda  
Our mission now is clear  
There is no longer need for fear.  
Legs for the lame man, eyes for the blind  
This you have given us because you're so kind.

mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim  
yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande paramānanda-mādhavam

**"I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains." (*****Cc. Madhya*****17.80)**

Although we are dumb and lame we are still shameless in our fallen condition. O Prabhupāda, we have no appreciation of your boundless glories. Your transcendental attributes are innumerable and unfathomable and to describe them all would be impossible. Nevertheless, we are trying to say something about your glorious qualities and activities as an offering on this Guru-pūjā day. We offer this to you for our purification and to express our humble gratitude for your causeless mercy.

These descriptions of ourselves as lame and dumb may appear to be poetic over-exaggerations to some, but actually we are dumb in that we are unable to speak anything of value. Before meeting Your Grace we could only make croaking sounds, possessing useless tongues exactly like the frogs—*jihvāsati dārdurikeva \[*SB 2.3.20\]. In a fool's paradise one fool will speak all nonsense in order to impress other fools with his so-called learning. So, that is one thing. But to speak something meaningful requires that one hear perfect instructions from the realized soul. By receiving instructions from you through your books, even the dumb can make eloquent speeches. This is due to your causeless mercy. Similarly we are also lame in that before receiving your mercy we could not accomplish anything beneficial. Before receiving your causeless mercy we were doing only harm to ourselves and others. With drugs, liquor and cigarettes, etc., we were destroying ourselves. And by meat-eating and the other sinful habits of illicit sex and gambling, we were causing so much pain to others. Our position was actually hopeless. We did not know the goal of life nor did we know how to achieve it. We did not know the consequences of sinful life nor did we know about the highest reward for practicing devotional service in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thus we did not know how to get out of material life.

*vidyāṁ cāvidyāṁ ca yas tad vedobhayaṁ saha*  
*avidyayā mṛtyuṁ tīrtvā vidyayāmṛtam aśnute*

**"Only one who can learn the process of nescience and that of transcendental knowledge side by side can transcend the influence of repeated birth and death and enjoy the full blessings of immortality." (*****Śrī Īśopaniṣad*** **11)**

But, by your powerful preaching, you have made us aware of both.

Western society is concerned with the body, and consequently we were always hankering and lamenting. Whether we liked that situation or not, we had to accept it. We were lame, unable to help ourselves and others, but you have engaged us in the highest welfare work: namely spreading the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and rescuing the conditioned souls from the clutches of *māyā*

Out of compassion for our suffering condition you have given the matchless gift of Lord Caitanya's divine love which we, unfortunately, take for granted. Our condition is like a delirious patient who is suffering from a severe illness. Not even recognizing the doctor as his friend due to madness, he fights with the doctor and tries to resist the medicine. But, as the doctor goes on administering the cure, so you also continue to show your mercy even though we are constantly offending your lotus feet. Thus you are showing us the real meaning of compassion.

As you more than once cited, *aśocyān anvaśocas tvaṁ prajñā-vādāṁś ca bhāṣase.* "While speaking learned words you are lamenting for what is not worthy of grief." Because of blindness people are following blind leaders who don't know the real aim of life. They are unnecessarily lamenting on the basis of the body. However, you are lamenting for what is actually worthy of grief. You are sorry for the suffering conditions of repeated birth and death. As you continue preaching regardless of your health, we can see your deep concern for all of us. We were previously taking this world to be everything, but by your association, gradually we are understanding that your lotus feet are everything.

*nāma-śreṣṭhaṁ manum api śacī-putram atra svarūpaṁ*  
*rūpaṁ tasyāgrajam uru-purīṁ māthurīṁ goṣṭhavātīm*  
*rādhā-kuṇḍaṁ giri-varam aho rādhikā-mādhavāśāṁ*  
*prāpto yasya prathita-kṛpaya śrī-guruṁ taṁ nato ’smi*

**"I bow down to the beautiful lotus feet of my spiritual master by whose causeless mercy I have received the supreme holy name, the divine mantra, the service of the son of Śacīmātā, the association of Svarūpa Dāmodara, Rūpa Gosvāmī, and his older brother Sanātana Gosvāmī, the supreme abode of Mathurā, the blissful abode of Vṛndāvana, the divine Rādhā Kuṇḍa and Govardhana Hill and the desire within my heart for the loving service of Śrī Rādhikā and Mādhava in Vṛndāvana."**

On this most auspicious day of your appearance we must thank you again and again for these matchless gifts. Although we are unqualified to serve you, we are simply praying that we may remain engaged at your lotus feet eternally.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

ISKCON New Delhi devotees:

## New Orleans

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We here at New Orleans Yatrā humbly pray for the intelligence and the strength to serve your divine self, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in a simple and offenseless way. Please be merciful unto us.

Lord Brahmā's majestic position as creator of the vast cosmic manifestation certainly must be respected by every single entity. Śiva's potency is so intense that simply by his dancing the entire universe is devastated. The king of the demigods Indradeva rides his celestial elephant-carrier Airāvata. Armed with his powerful thunderbolts he can easily knock off the peaks of the greatest mountains. Vivasvān the sun-god lights and heats the extensive expansions of space as he smoothly makes his route in his effulgent chariot. The goddess of fortune Lakṣmī-devī is so enticing that just to have her association even the most reputed gentleman will engage in the most abominable activities. See him madly struggling to have her under his control, although he's actually under hers.

There are thirty-three million demigods and they are deputed by KṚṢṆA to see that the universe is properly facilitated. Their positions of control are far superior to the other innumerable conditioned souls. Yet all of them are trapped in temporary bodies stuck within the thick coverings of the universe.

Gurudeva! All these demigods are great masters but they all bow down to your lotus feet. Śrī Guru, you are the sum total of all the demigods, capable of delivering anyone within the fourteen planetary systems. Therefore we offer you our most respectful obeisances and beg you to keep us with you.

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have told us the secrets of time and space. Everyone is puzzled at the idea of life beyond, but you are showing us everything, taking us everywhere. The universal activities are so amazing and wonderful that simply by investigating these cosmic affairs one becomes aghast. But actually it's all a dream... a colossal phantasmagoria, *bhūtvā bhūtvā pralīyate \[*Bg. 8.19\].

*ā-brahma-bhuvanāl lokāḥ*  
*punar āvartino 'rjuna*  
*mām upetya tu kaunteya*  
*punar janma na vidyate*  
*Bg 8.16*

**"From the highest planet in the material world down to the lowest, all are places of misery wherein repeated birth and death take place, but one who attains to My abode, O son of Kuntī, never takes birth again.**

*Jayate jagad-guru!* Your glories are sung throughout the three worlds. You have saved us from this painful Ferris wheel-like transmigration of one body to the next, one planet to the next. Whether we are high or low, happy or distressed, in this body or that, it doesn't matter because it's all hellish. Our position now is that we are most thankful and forever indebted to you for taking us off this universal merry-go-round.

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**"The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as a raincloud pours water on a forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master delivers the materially afflicted world by extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master who is an ocean of auspicious qualities."**

O master, we've tried and tried and tried like imbeciles to enjoy the miragelike variegatedness of this great manifestation. We've been through it all in this lifetime and in so many millions of lifetimes before. Now finally disgusted and exhausted we are through trying. You are so munificent that you were right there just when we needed you, just when we wanted you. By your causeless mercy our cosmic dream is over. You have awakened us to the reality of KṚṢṆA. *Vāsudevaḥ sarvam iti \[*Bg. 7.19\]. There is nothing else.

*Brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva* \[*Cc.* Madhya 19.151\]. While wandering throughout the universe in so many species of life, if one is very fortunate he gets the seed of the creeper of devotion by the mercy of KṚṢṆA and *guru*.

Without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are lost. Please help us to hang on to your lotus feet. Praying for just one drop of your ocean of mercy, repeatedly we fall at your lotus feet. Jaya Prabhupāda!!!

Ānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Vaṁśīmohana dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Rick / Guṇavāsa dāsa brahmacārī / Vedānta-kṛt dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Rick / Jñānanatha dāsa brahmacārī / Raṅganātha dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Ed / Cārupa dāsa brahmacārī / Vṛkodara dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Tim Jvarajit dāsa brahmacārī / Muralīmanohara dāsa / Mr. Jack Prabhu Kālākṣara dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Jim / Śanti-devī dāsī Paramātmā dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta David / Premamayī-devī dāsī Rāsavilāsa dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Don / Kanyā Kumārī-devī dāsī

## New Vrindaban

Representative of Kṛṣṇa's mercy  
our dear friend you strive to help us remember.  
Your purity opens us to this eternal fire  
of sacrifice to the Lord, then increasing  
like a ray of light expanding never ceasing.

This energy is yours to rule  
for you are Kṛṣṇa's ruled in sublime affection.

Gurudeva of compassion  
fulfilling the desire of the Supreme Truth  
your words are like jewels of effulgent fire  
scattering the jackals of matter  
that devour the breath of the soul.

O master  
walking on this once barren land  
holy dust, now holy dhāma  
teaching us to live in simplicity  
to see Kṛṣṇa everywhere in a special way  
for there is magic in His dealings.

By your grace one gets Kṛṣṇa  
delivering Rādhā Vṛndāvana Candra  
for our eyes and hearts to focus on  
capturing the inmates with Their beauty.

Only by your grace, because you have captured Him  
a friend to this art of eternal rasa,  
Can we surrender enough to hold  
a second of this masterpiece?

When will you come and bless us  
by living in your palace, Śrīla Prabhupāda?  
It is a creation of your heart  
for us to offer ours.

If you stay for one night  
or a million years  
this palace, these souls  
are eternally yours  
to glorify the most beautiful  
cowherd boy of Vṛndāvana.

**-**Inmates of New Vrindaban

## New York

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

With the morning sunshine streaming onto the veranda in Māyāpur, you declared that place to be Vaikuṇṭha. "Where else can one always hear Hare Kṛṣṇa?" you said. When asked about your health, you said, "I am not feeling very well these days. Actually I am not fit to even be here." Yet actually you were the only one qualified to be there. The only intelligent man is one who considers his own material intelligence to be useless and submissively accepts that knowledge emanating from the Supreme Personality of Godhead through the via media of the *paramparā*. You are completely transcendental yet display the humility befitting one of the countless fallen conditioned living entities embarrassingly caught in the material existence.

You criticize the scientists and educators who ever-so-humbly recommend themselves to be lords and masters of all knowledge, although they are devoid of the most basic understanding of the process of self-realization. You say that we do not even need to argue with them about any points of religion, since we can defeat them on the basis of the most simple principles of mechanical engineering. With all their wrenches, graphs, dials, and sophisticated modern equipment, they cannot meet your challenge to just repair a broken machine, namely, a dead body, so that it will get up and walk and speak again. In that way you challenge them and show that their knowledge is nothing but rascaldom and show that the real means of accepting knowledge is related directly by the perfect seer of all things, Lord Kṛṣṇa.

You said that one should be like Sanātana Goswāmī and approach the Supreme Personality of Godhead in all humility, acknowledging one's own lack of realization of one's original spiritual identity.

*āpanāra hitāhita kichui nā jāni!*  
*grāmya-vyavahāre paṇḍita, tāi satya māni*  
*kṛpā kari' yadi more kariyācha uddhāra*  
*āpana-kṛpāte kaha 'kartavya' āmāra*  
*'ke āmi', 'kene āmāya jāre tāpa-traya'*  
*ihā nāhi jāni—'kemane hita haya'*  
*'sādhya'-'sādhana'-tattva puchite nā jāni*  
*kṛpā kari' saba tattva kaha ta' āpani"*

**"I do not know what is beneficial for me and what is detrimental. Nonetheless, in ordinary dealings people consider me a learned scholar, and I am also thinking of myself as such. Out of Your causeless mercy, You have delivered me from the materialistic path. Now, by the same causeless mercy, please tell me what my duty is. Who am I? Why do the threefold miseries always give me trouble? If I do not know this, how can I be benefited? Actually I do not know how to inquire about the goal of life and process for obtaining it. Being merciful upon me, please explain all these truths." (*****Cc. Madhya-līlā*** **20.100-103)**

Sanātana Gosvāmī was the most exalted devotee of Caitanya Mahāprabhu and was qualified by humility, knowledge, and purity. You are just like Sanātana in all these qualities, yet you possess even more bountiful mercy. When Caitanya Mahāprabhu ordered Candraśekhara to go outside his door and greet the devotee standing there and bring him in for an audience, Candraśekhara Ācārya could not even recognize Sanātana as anything more than a "Muslim mendicant." But due to Sanātana's great devotion, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu seated him in an elevated place right next to Himself and embraced him, recognizing him as the greatest of all Vaiṣṇavas present.

Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is called *mahā-vadānya-avatāra*, the most merciful. You, the devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, are more merciful than Kṛṣṇa Himself. Although Sanātana Gosvāmī was qualified to some degree by his transcendental characteristics, we have no such qualification. We are refugees from a sinful world where animal slaughter is a way of life, speculation is an honored principle, illicit sex life is an economic convenience, and intoxication is the only solace. Yet somehow or other you are willing to give us shelter and take us into the association of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

We are so weak in our ability to execute the principles of *bhakti-yoga* that we are very easily influenced by the material energy, and therefore we are called marginal, or *tatastha-śakti.* We are like that uncertain area between the seawater and the dry sand on the beach, which is sometimes moistened or even drenched by the waves of the ocean of material existence, and which sometimes gleams in the bright midday sunlight of uncontaminated spiritual existence. We are affected by the demands and designs of materialistic persons and have the tendency to fall prey to their wiles—sometimes grossly and sometimes more subtly—by allowing ourselves to become influenced by the preaching of atheistic, demoniac scientists, politicians, and philosophers of the modern age. Although you are preaching only on the platform of perfect realization and can perfectly describe the sure means of escaping the pounding waves of material existence, we still become affected by the mundane intellectuals and scholars who try to rationalize everything and see this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement as just one more tiny eruption in the placid continuum of social awareness. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are able to discern the real truth and therefore, being uninfluenced by such rascals, always instruct us in the art of pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

You purify our cluttered minds and filthy hearts—preaching like your Guru Mahārāja, Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself—on how to take up the bow on the battlefield and fight at the divine behest. When we might think to make concessions or accommodations to the social conventions of the material world, you encourage us to fight, to give up attachments to fathers, sons, mothers, cousin-brothers, and teachers in order to execute the will of the Supreme Lord. Although we want to cling to our material existence, you cut us away and instead put us in the association of Arjuna on the battlefield, kneeling before Lord Kṛṣṇa, bearing His chastisement and taking up the fight. In material life there is nothing but degradation and ignominy, couched within birth, death, old age, and painful disease. But you are giving us the glory of the fight rather than the placid indolence found in dying old dogs.

According to *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, the farther west one travels the more demoniac the people become. We are so far away from Māyāpur and the abode of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, lost in the far reaches of the West, and therefore we are more demoniac than even the infamous Jagai and Mādhāi, who were so degraded as to even offend the most merciful Nityānanda Prabhu. Yet still you are saving us and guiding us back to the spiritual world. No one could sing of your glories enough; no one can offer you enough homage. You have descended swanlike in the Western world, at ease even in the most hellish conditions provided by Kṛṣṇa's most expert assistant, Māyādevī herself. Because you are never forgetful of Kṛṣṇa, you give the only constructive knowledge and the only real help. Without you our lives are useless, tormented, and hopeless; but serving you, we can aspire for the spiritual world. Unlike Sanātana, who admitted his ignorance and begged for instruction in the science of the self (*ātma-vidyā*), we are interested only in showing our pitiful expertise in perfecting our material sense gratification. Yet still you gently chide us, entreating, "When will you let me set you free?"

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you alone know Kṛṣṇa's plans and what is within our hearts. We will wander aimlessly if we do not have your command. Please do with us as you will.

*Your servants at ISKCON New York,*
