## Niṭai Gaura Traveling World Saṅkīrtana Party (Kolhapur (India)

*Vyāsa-pūjā offering by the devotees of the bullock cart Saṅkīrtana at the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please kindly accept my most prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet. Unlimited glories to you. Your Divine Grace. The only reason I am in this unwanted existence is that I have misused the minute independence given to me by Kṛṣṇa. I have been trying my best to lord over the material world from time immemorial. But each time the Lord's external energy, *māyā*, was kicking me. And the Lord ultimately made me *bhāgyavān*, fortunate, and has brought me to your lotus feet. You are the external manifestation of Paramātmā, and you are the representative of the Lord. Now I am totally at your mercy. And now I do not know anyone else except you as my lord and the controller of all my wanderings.

Once upon a time I was situated on the spiritual planet where the predominating Deity is the Lord Himself. And now once again I am situated on the ISKCON planet, where you are the predominating deity. But unfortunately I am the same person with the same age-old propensity of being independent. So now at this stage I repeatedly pray at your lotus feet—O vice-lord, be kind enough to take my minute independence away. Do not offer me the choice but your service, I have no power to discriminate and do not have any understanding of my own self interest, *na te viduḥ svārtha-gatiṁ hi viṣṇum* (*SB* 7.5.31). So I beg that you make me your slave eternally and do not allow me to speculate or make plans of my own. I fell down from the spiritual sky and now your mercy manifestation ISKCON has picked me up. But if I fall down from the ISKCON planet there is no other hope for me. Let all my own plans go to hell. Let only your plans and work get done. Let only your desire get fulfilled. And as an obedient slave let your desire become my desire.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please fulfill this, the only desire of mine, and no more.

*Always praying this*, We eternally remain your servants,

Lokanātha Svāmī / Ātmatattva / Jagat Vicitra dāsa brahmacārī Kṛṣṇadāsa Vaidya dāsa brahmacārī / Abhayāśraya dāsa brahmacārī / Līlā Kamala dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛṣṇa Prema dāsa brahmacārī / Purī dāsa brahmacārī / Ravi dāsa brahmacārī / Rohiṇītanaya dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Vilāsa

## Ottawa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our eternal obeisances at your divine lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, where even the most fallen of sinful beings can be accepted by your overwhelming compassion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we seldom have personal association with you, still you are giving us your association through your writings and teachings, and in these books we find, your presence. Every word you speak is so full of devotional inspiration that even the most sinful of people can be enlivened to engage in devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Now we simply have to perfect the art of hearing from you.

We find that the more we associate ourselves with you through your books and devotional service through your grace, the more we want others to also come and hear your every word. And as they realize your exalted position as spiritual master of the universe and take to your lotus feet as their only possible shelter, we all become joyful to see you increasingly glorified. Your Divine Grace should be worshiped by every sane human being, and it is our duty to see that this happens. We find that as people read your books, their understanding and appreciation for you increases and so does ours. So we shall attempt to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to all corners by your instruction and permission with increased book distribution. We hope that you shall derive some amount of pleasure from our meager offerings at your feet.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, allow us the intelligence to appreciate our position as your eternal servants and in this mood continue working very honestly and hard to distribute the highest knowledge to all humanity in the form of your books. Kindly permit us to continue trying to please you through our service, as imperfect as it may be. We beg to remain your humble servants.

The disciples of your Ottawa center,

Gaura dāsa brahmacārī / Rūpa Vilāsa dāsa adhikārī / Vāruṇī-devī dāsī Lakṣmīnātha dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Denis / Mitravindā-devī dāsī / Nirantara dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Robert / Bhaktin Sue Kṛṣṇadāsa dāsa adhikārī / Paurṇamāsī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Diane

## Paris

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

**We offer our respectful obeisances unto our spiritual master. His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is so dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter of the Lord's lotus feet.**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**Our respectful obeisances unto you, O servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya and delivering the Western world from voidism and impersonalism.**

*śrī-guru-carana-padma, kevala bhakati-sadma,*  
*bando mui sāvadhāna mate*  
*jāhāra prasāde bhāi, e bha va toriyā jāi,*  
*krṣṇa-prāpti hoy jāhā hāte*

**"The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can attain pure devotional service. I bow to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence. By his grace one can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa."**

It is impossible to describe just how exalted is the position of the spiritual master. We must first seriously understand how small and insignificant we are and, upon understanding this, surrender completely to our beloved spiritual master. Only at this time do our lives become of value.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you have come to save the entire world. Sanātana Gosvāmī humbly submitted to Lord Caitanya that, "People consider me to be a very wise man, but I do not know who I am, where I come from, or where I shall go after death." This is exactly our position because we also did not have any knowledge of our real nature. Your spiritual master, His Divine Grace Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, said that this material world is not a fit place for a gentleman. Anybody in this world, trapped within a material body, has to suffer the threefold miseries in different forms of distress until such time that he reaches the point of complete surrender to the bona fide spiritual master.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**"I was born in the darkness of ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torchlight of knowledge. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master."**

Only by your grace can one become freed from this false conception of identifying with the material body. We were all so proud of this miserable body, worshiping it and giving it our complete attention, without knowing that it is just a bag of stool, blood, urine, mucus and bile. There is nothing glamorous about it at all.

By your causeless mercy you have come to deliver the fallen souls of this Kali-yuga, so we can rightfully say that you are *suhṛdaṁ sarva-bhūtānām \[*Bg. 5.29\], or the friend of all living entities, who can deliver us from all material miseries. In this age of Kali, the qualities of mercifulness, truthfulness, cleanliness and austerity are almost lost and it would have been impossible for any of us to become freed from the clutches of *māyā* if you had not come to save us. By your causeless mercy you have made us the most fortunate by creating ISKCON so that we can have *sādhu-saṅga* to protect us from *māyā*.

*sādhu-saṅga-kṛpā kimvā kṛṣṇera kṛpāya*  
*kāmādi 'duḥsaṅga' chāḍi' śuddha-bhakti pāya*

**"One is elevated to the platform of devotional life by the mercy of the Vaiṣṇava, the bona fide spiritual master, and by the special mercy of Kṛṣṇa. On that platform one gives up all material desires and the association of unwanted persons. Thus one is elevated to the platform of pure devotional service." (*****Cc. Madhya*** **24.97)**

The *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.23.56) states:

*neha yat karma dharmāya na virāgāya kalpate*  
*na tīrtha-pada-sevāyai jīvann api mṛto hi saḥ*

**"A person whose work does not lead him to spiritual life, a person whose spiritual rituals do not lead him to renunciation or a person situated in renunciation that does not lead him to devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, must be considered as a dead man in spite of the fact that he is living."**

So, the ultimate aim of the human form of life is found in the unalloyed devotional service to the Lord, and this goal we have to reach in this very life. It is only by your grace that one can reach this perfection, and on this day of Vyāsa-pūjā we all bow down at your lotus feet and pray to obtain your mercy.

You are the perfect *ācārya* because your very life shows us that pure devotional service cannot be checked by any material conditions. In any condition, whether it be advanced age or temporary illness, you have proved that pure devotional service is directly under the protection of Kṛṣṇa's divine energy.

By your example we must now have faith in your instructions, and it is the duty of every one of us to follow in your footsteps.

**ALL GLORIES TO YOU, Śrīla Prabhupāda!**

*Your fallen servants at Paris ISKCON*

## Philadelphia

*Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. When you arrived in Philadelphia in the summer of 1975, we drove you from the airport in a hired Cadillac limousine; this was noted by an envious reporter, who later challenged you: "Why did you ride in a Cadillac limousine from the airport?" You replied: "My disciples have provided me with this, limousine, but actually, it is not at all good enough. They have not done very well by me. *Sākṣād dharitvena samasta-śāstrair uktas thatā bhavyata eva sadbhiḥ*," you quoted. "The spiritual master is to be worshiped on an equal level with God. Because the spiritual master is the most confidential servant of God and is therefore very dear to him, he receives the same honor that is paid to the Supreme Lord. God rides in a chariot of gold, so my disciples should have provided a golden car for me. This Cadillac is not at all sufficient.”

The reporter sat there speechless, but the devotees from Philadelphia were feeling ashamed. How far short we fall in every way! It's true: you do deserve a car of gold. No service we perform, no praise we offer, no surrender we proffer, comes even respectably close to what, by rights, ought to be given to you. We had even been feeling somewhat proud of the hired Cadillac limousine; so often, indeed, we have found ourselves thinking, "Now I have performed some real service for Śrīla Prabhupāda," or "Now I have really surrendered to His Divine Grace," or similar inglorious notions.

But how can we ever render unto you the service that we owe? Our debt cannot be discharged, except by further borrowing. We have discovered that when we attempt some service, unless we obtain your mercy, we cannot properly perform it. Without your grace, we always fail. Thus we simply become increasingly entangled in the network of indebtedness, and we cannot escape your grip; thus we remain forever your bound servants.

Neither are we able to mint with our dull and earthy tongues the sterling and golden words that piled in heaps still would not exhaust the value of your priceless worth.

Forever indebted, perpetually embarrassed, we cannot pay the price for your matchless gifts.

But still we can dream... that one day will come when the bedazzled crowds at the airport will see you mount a car of burnished gold, flaming like the sun, surrounded by throngs of purified chanters, chanting your glories in exquisite verse. The golden chariot shall carry you down a road inlaid with gems, over a carpet of stemless fragrant flowers. Rows of flowering trees will line the way, forming a perfumed canopy overhead, and there will be lakes of sweet-smelling water like nectar, with jeweled bathing places. Flocks of birds will pour out their songs in ecstasy, and the shining silk banners, proclaiming your glories, shall sway in the cool, scented breezes.

In this we only could hope to begin to receive you properly. And what would the reporters have to say then?

*Your most fallen servants in*Philadelphia

## Pittsburgh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is stated in your *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*: Out of many millions of living entities wandering throughout the creation of Lord Brahmā, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with the bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service.

Somehow or other you have chosen us as your disciples, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even though we have no qualifications and our hearts are covered with deep layers of material dust, still you so patiently guide us on the path back to Godhead. Our conditioning and association in this material world had us convinced that intoxication, illicit sex life, eating animal flesh and gambling would give us the highest taste. This taste cannot be found anywhere in this material world. No amount of money, fame or learning can have it. Only by the grace of the pure devotee of the Lord can one be given the highest state—Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Our thank you seems so insincere and insignificant, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We can only pray to Kṛṣṇa to please keep us eternally engaged in your service for only by your grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can we escape the miserable condition of repeated birth and death.

*Your eternal servants,*

## Portland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

A long with all our wonderful Godbrothers throughout the world, we, the residents of New Maṇipura Dhāma, prostrate ourselves in the dust of your lotus feet, begging for your mercy and inspiration to assist you in pushing on the great s*aṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. O divine master, please forgive all our offenses, rectify our faults and remove the snake of envy from our hearts. O merciful Gurudeva, please bestow upon us enthusiasm, determination, and patience, the jewels of pure devotion. Infuse us with your compassion, O beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, for this is the loving quality of the true Vaiṣṇava. Shower upon us, O ocean of mercy, the spirit of cooperation that we may most effectively spread this most important movement. O *siṁha-guru*, most of all give us the strength and courage to continue on against all obstacles in this dangerous age of Kali. Please don't let us fall, O merciful spiritual master. Outside of serving you, there is nothing else to do with our lives. Now we surrender unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please give us the shelter of your lotus feet!

*Begging to remain your servants*in New Manipura

## Port Royal

Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Of all your qualities, I would like to praise your ability to look at the world and genuinely appreciate the good in it. Generally, devotees are swanlike because they want to serve Kṛṣṇa. This service to Kṛṣṇa is the essence of life. The swan has the ability to extract the essence, the milk, from a mixture of milk and water. So I can see, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you are *paramahaṁsa*, the supreme swanlike devotee.

You are traveling through this material planet, simply extracting its essence, the eternal relationship of service between the people and Kṛṣṇa. You have explained that "Kṛṣṇa is so kind and favorable that if a servitor is accused even of great offenses, Kṛṣṇa does not take this into consideration. He simply considers the service that is rendered by His devotee." You are obviously acting on Kṛṣṇa's behalf in this matter, and by your example, you are giving us a taste of real love, the only foundation on which we can build our Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Love is manifested in the desire to serve the beloved. That is your teaching, the essence of spiritual life. *Jīvera 'svarūpa' haya-kṛṣṇera 'nitya dāsa*' *\[Cc.* Madhya 20.108\]. Never mind our crowlike tendencies, if we can just get firmly fixed up in some service to your lotus feet, we will be rightly situated.

*Your fallen servants,*New Varṣaṇa Farm

## Potomac

Oh, compassionate Guru  
More welcome than the sun  
Shine upon our hearts  
Till our ignorance is done.

Your words have such power  
They free men who truly hear  
From scientific serfdom  
Ignorance and fear.

You are the impetus,  
Enthusing us-Go on  
Increasing the flood  
Of Śrī-Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana.

Please never leave us  
Bereft of your strong embrace  
Our lives have no meaning  
Minus Your Divine Grace.

We beg to offer our assembled lives  
like straws before your holy feet.

Your disciples at New Hastināpura

## Puerto Rico

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Even as I sit to write  
these words  
under the evening light  
the mind puts up a tremendous fight.

It concocts the paltry conception  
of possibly gaining recognition  
for a masterful poetic creation.

No matter the activity performed  
the mind is incessantly torn  
apart  
by these impure desires.  
It seeks, like a miser, to be adorned  
With the glass jewels of fame, adoration  
and distinction.

These three baubles I don't admire  
but their presence arouses that impelling desire  
to be crowned as Īśvara.

Coronated as "I am" (in existence) apart from Kṛṣṇa  
and attempting to establish this fact  
this false king (the mind) tries to preserve what  
he has  
and independently carry what he lacks.

But that burden of illusion is great  
and my feet are slipping under the heavy weight.  
The only thing which can change this fate  
is the mercy-stick of support  
from Śrī Gurudeva.

Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
with your smiling lotus face  
and swanlike grace  
you extend that rod  
with a reassuring nod  
and encourage all to take hold.

Your unlimited mercy one cannot estimate  
yet this is but one of your unlimited traits  
of many that you possess.

Śrīla Prabhupāda  
I'm simply praying  
despite the asslike braying  
of this untamed mind  
that I may hear your words  
with an open ear  
and lock them in this leaden heart  
then instead of speculation  
draw on the truth you impart  
that Kṛṣṇa is God  
and I His servant.

Your eternal servants at Puerto Rico

## Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara TSKP

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda*

You are the General Directing the fight  
Revealing the Godhead  
Destroying māyās' might.

Like Arjuna, we fear the battle call  
We hesitate—we tremble—we fall.  
Like Lord Kṛṣṇa, you lift us up by your instruction  
You chastise—you enlighten you save us from destruction.

Remembering the cowherd boys, fearless we march  
Into the Aghasurine cities and towns  
Defying their 'fishy bad smells' and  
Skyscraper fangs touching the clouds.

Armed with your books, forward we thrust  
Deep into māyā's kingdom,  
Illusions dispelled, the conditioned souls  
Experience real freedom.

Māyā advanced through the godless technological race  
Her gleam and glitter turn men's eyes from God's grace  
Fouling the air, poisoning rivers and lakes  
The scientists are modern Kāliya snakes.

And with your strong words  
You kick on their heads  
Replacing their venom  
With nectar instead.

O Master! O Lord!  
Please show us the arts  
Of chanting and dancing  
And purifying our hearts!

"Jaya Prabhupāda" and "Hare Kṛṣṇa"  
These our battle cry! Pray to remember  
Your lotus feet when we die.

And in your monumental plan  
To create a new history  
Remember your words—"Without a hard struggle—  
What is the chance for a glorious Victory."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you. How can I glorify you adequately? Only by your mercy will it be possible for me to begin. Like Haridāsa Thākura, you are an ocean of transcendental qualities. Like Vāsudeva Datta, your compassion knows no bounds as you are prepared to bear the sins of the universe and go to hell for Lord Śrī Caitanya. As Vakreśvara Paṇḍita danced all night, you once danced for Lord Jagannātha's entire festival in London, giving the Lord and all your devotees great joy. Just to hear this is wonderful. Who can estimate your fame? It is spread all over the three worlds. Like Rūpa and Sanātana, you are increasing the limits of the ocean of love of Godhead. Tirelessly preaching like no one else, you are never-endingly speaking of Lord Caitanya's mission, inspiring all of your devotees to preach this *nāma-saṅkīrtana*. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, so great you are, you have saved the world with your Bhaktivedanta purports.

You are my heart, my life and soul. My morning rising is by your call of service to the transcendental Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Morning bath, *viṣṇu-tilaka*, saffron robes, *Gayatrī*, thousands of Godbrothers, *japa* and *maṅgala-ārātrika* are all from you; priceless gifts of service and association you have bestowed. The Deities and Their *prasāda*, more than we can eat, and a first-class transcendental home; you have offered all this. What I could not have dreamed of in eternity, what would have been impossible for me to conceive of with this mundane mind, into this ISKCON Kṛṣṇa consciousness I have gained entrance by your grace. You are His Divine Grace, the mercy incarnation of Kṛṣṇa, and this movement is a manifestation of your pure, uncontaminated desire to please Śrī Gaurasundara.

This mission, your life and soul and every breath, is pure nectar, simply sublime and full of transcendental ecstasy. To take part as your assistant birth after birth—let that be my only desire. By assisting your preaching I can taste the highest bliss and know who are Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa—your youthful loving couple for whom you've given up this world in madness. You are Their Lordship's eternal servitor and topmost devotee, and for this I have the proof. For Them you have come here and saved me and all of us. You are making everyone a Vaiṣṇava. Who can resist you? It is not possible. You have defeated everyone. You have taught the world real science, the best economy, perfect government and better business. You are the greatest cook, the best musician and sweetest vocalist. Even Nārada and Vyāsadeva sing your glories. You are the greatest author, greatest devotee, greatest spiritual master of all time, yet more humble than a blade of grass. Tolerating my offenses, always encouraging me, O Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am your dog. I cannot begin to glorify you yet you have allowed me to write some words. I love you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let me distribute your books wherever you see fit. You are my guide, my master, and my life is all your mercy. I am falling at your lotus feet, please consider my plea. You are my only shelter, what can I do? I am appealing to you and you will not turn me away. You have given me this *sannyāsa* order of life, now again I promise you, I make this vow, eternally I am your menial servant.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to you!

**Śrī Śrī Radha Damodara Traveling Saṅkīrtana Party**

## Rome

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Narottama dāsa Thākura says that the spiritual master is *karuṇā-sindhu,* an ocean of mercy. You are an unlimited ocean of mercy, and there is no person more glorious than you in all the three worlds. Out of compassion you have come into this world of fallen souls to bring us back to Godhead, although we don't have any qualities. Just as the sun can extract water even from stool and transform it into pure drops of rain, you extracted us from our stoollike lives full of sinful activities and impersonalism and elevated us to a position enviable even by the demigods. It is said that even great *devas* desire to take birth as devotees in this movement, and you, with your mercy, have transformed such fallen souls like us into devotees.

Gurudeva! As the sunlight is more potent when it goes through a lens, in the same way Kṛṣṇa's mercy is greater when it goes through you. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is glorified because He saved Jagāi and Mādhāi, whose activities were just like childish pranks in comparison with our abominable sins. Caitanya Mahāprabhu has saved with His mercy Jagāi and Mādhāi, but that same mercy, filtered through you, has increased millions of times, therefore you are now saving hundreds of thousands of Jagāis and Mādhāis.

In *Kṛṣṇa Book* there is a description of autumn in which it explains how the earth becomes luxuriant after the rainy season. Likewise, the devotees, after joining the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, become bright and look like they're coming directly from Vaikuṇṭha. But just like the earth becomes dry during the summer season, we also become dry and lose our qualities if we forget your instruction, because these qualities are not ours but are only a reflection of some of your unlimited qualities.

*yogyatā-vicāre, kichu nāhi pāi,*  
*tomāra karuṇā-sāra*  
*karuṇā nā hoile, kāṅdiyā kāṅdiyā,*  
*prāṇa nā rākhibo āra*

Gurudeva! As the lotus flower grows in the mud but is never touched by it, you stay in the mud of this material world but you are never contaminated. Your lotus eyes see always Kṛṣṇa, your lotus lips chant always Kṛṣṇa's glories, your lotus hands are always engaged in Kṛṣṇa's service, your lotus feet dance always for the Lord, and your lotus mind is always fixed on the Lord. When Kṛṣṇa plays His flute, the waves of the Yamunā carry lotus flowers to His feet. In the same way, your mind always brings thoughts of love to Kṛṣṇa.

*ohe! vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse karuṇā kori’*  
*diyā pada-chāyā, śodho he āmāya,*  
*tomāra caraṇa dhori*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our only hope is to get hold of your lotus feet; we cannot do anything else but pray to always have your mercy and be able to follow in your footsteps. *Māyā's* attacks are so strong that we can fall down any day. Gurudeva, give us a drop of your mercy so that we can resist *māyā's* attacks. Make our ears constantly full of your instructions and inundated with Kṛṣṇa's pastimes so that they will not be attracted by the whispers of *māyā*. Please, enter into our rebellious minds and make them full of thoughts about Kṛṣṇa so that they will not be attracted by the waves of desires. Impress your holy image in our eyes so that they will not be attracted by *māyā's* winkings. Give us the strength to engage our hands in chanting the *japa-mālā* that you have given us and in performing devotional activities, otherwise these hands will be engaged in caressing *māyā*. Give us the enthusiasm so that our legs will be always engaged in dancing in front of the Deities (which are manifested due to your mercy) and for running from one person to another in order to distribute your books so that our legs will not be engaged in running after *māyā*. Give us a drop of your mercy so that our tongue can really taste the transcendental sound of the *maha-māntra* and the nectarean flavor of *prasāda*. In this way we will lose the desire to taste the garbage of the material world. Give us the strength to engage all our energies in distributing your books. By doing this we will not waste our energies foolishly in sense gratification.

*nāmnām akāri bahudhā nija-sarva-śaktis*  
*tatrārpitā niyamitaḥ smaraṇe na kālaḥ*  
*etādṛśī tava kṛpā bhagavan mamāpi*  
*durdaivam īdṛśam ihājani nānurāgaḥ*  
*Cc. Antya 20.16*

**"O my Lord, Your holy name alone can render all benediction to living beings, and thus You have hundreds and millions of names, like Kṛṣṇa and Govinda. In these transcendental names You have invested all Your transcendental energies. There are not even hard and fast rules for chanting these names. O my Lord, out of kindness You enable us to easily approach You by Your holy names, but I am so unfortunate that I have no attraction for them."**

You are always deeply absorbed in tasting the ecstasy of chanting the glories of Lord Caitanya, so we just pray to you to allow us to taste one drop of the ocean of bliss you constantly feel, so that we can keep chanting the holy name *Yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi*: without your blessing we are completely lost and helpless. Without you we would still go here and there barking like dogs searching for some illusory, ephemeral pleasure: we would still be in the stool like hogs, thinking ourselves happy.

Gurudeva! Without your presence the three worlds would be completely empty. We invoke your merciful glance upon us: please give us the strength to follow you and to push on this Saṅkīrtana movement.

Gurudeva! We have no words to describe your glories. Śrīla Prabhupāda, the more we serve you, the more we realize your greatness and also our insignificance.

From Rome, the very same empire of ignorance and vice, your disciples prostrate themselves at your lotus feet, calling for your protection, O emperor of gentleness, king of self-control, austerity, purity, tolerance, honesty, wisdom, knowledge and piety. You are the emperor of the real world of bliss, please help us to get out of this realm of māyā.

## San Diego

*Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

While observing how all the countless species of life herein are being so greatly pained by this duality of the material world, we have begun to appreciate how many millions of lifetimes of suffering we have spent transmigrating from body to body in this miserable place.

Having finally collapsed at your lotus feet, we are realizing how essential is the association of your devotees, without whom we would continue to seek reciprocation with the living dead.

... and your service, bereft of which we'd be perpetually drained by the demands of our senses, and engulfed in the concoctions of our devious minds.

... and your books, without which we'd have no more to utter than sound vibrations which had previously driven us all crazy.

Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda, anything of value that we think, feel, or do is only because of your unlimited mercy upon us.

We realize, also, that any expression of thanks is but a mere fragment of the gratitude we should actually be feeling. Therefore, we must also beg forgiveness for our innumerable offenses that are keeping us from properly appreciating our relationship with you. And we pray that some-day we may become true *bhaktas* at your lotus feet, and help fulfill your most cherished desire to reunite the people of the world at the lotus feet of your most beloved Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*Your most fallen servants,*San Diego Yātrā

## Santa Cruz, Bolivia

The spiritual master is the eternal associate of the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and by his causeless mercy only is one able to embark upon the path of devotional service. A moment's association with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can cancel out the miseries of unending births in *saṁsāra*, and so I am only praying that one so foolish and fallen as I can come to know you, if but for only a moment. I long to have eyes anointed with the pure love necessary to see you. And when I come to know you, then I will also know Kṛṣṇa because you are always situated at His lotus feet. Please let me go with you, for I want to serve you always, whether you are present before me or we are separated in love. You are my eternal spiritual master, the eternal companion of my soul, and my only refuge in this chaos of material existence. I beg to kick this grotesque, distorted false ego to pieces and cry to serve you in sincerity. But for you, life is nothing more than an unending, screaming nightmare existence. Please engage me eternally and thus keep me always near you.

*Your eternal servants* at Santa Cruz, Bolivia

## Santiago

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

The debtor born of parents—obliged to those who have given him life. Material obligations in family, friendship, society, and love tie him, fry him, gag and bind him. And he dies! Drowned in the obligation ocean. *Brahmāṇda bhramite—*frustrated, wandering fool. *Kona bhāgyavān jīva—*then by good fortune he is born again. *Guru Kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya*. His father teaches him to walk and talk, to live and love, and in so doing gives the most marvelous gift, something always wanted, wondrous gift of light—*Bhakti-latā-bīja—*KṚṢṆA. The gift is real, the obligation too. What do we owe to him who has given us reality? Where is repayment's refuge? "Take the gift," he says, "*accept* the gift," he says, "that is how to repay me." Here is an eternal, blissful debt we can never repay; but let us dedicate our eternal existence to the task—the endless obligation that makes the debtor a saintly son.

## Santo Domingo

**"Any person who seriously desires to achieve real happiness must seek out a bona fide spiritual master and take shelter of him by initiation." (*****Bhāg*****11.3.21)**

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We beg you to accept our prostrate obeisances at your divine lotus feet. O divine lord and beloved master, we don't know how to honor you properly; we pray you to excuse this incapacity. Please give us the power to properly honor all living beings. Please give us the power to understand your supreme glories.

Dear spiritual father, we are devoid of all strength and intelligence. We are very low and fallen. How can such persons honor and explain the qualities of a great *paramahaṁsa* and sublime Vaiṣṇava like you? But somehow or other we have gained shelter at your divine lotus feet, due to your great mercy upon all the conditioned souls.

O venerable Vaiṣṇava, we beg to remain in your service,

Your servants,

Rādhā-śaraṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Mīnākṣī dāsa brahmacārī / Amala Harināma dāsa brahmacārī / Viprahita dāsa brahmacārī / Bīr Candra dāsa brahmacārī / Jyotsna dāsa brahmacārī / Lalita Govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Viśvadeva dāsa brahmacārī / Rocismān dāsa brahmacārī / Prayāga Pāvana dāsa brahmacārī / Nemicakra dāsa brahmacārī / Vadīrāja dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Dhimas / Bhakta Ramon / Bhakta Guelo / Bhakta Enrique / Vīrāya-devī dāsī / Pranada-devī dāsī

## Sao Paulo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On the occasion of this Vyāsa-pūja day, we desire to declare our complete dependence upon your transcendental mercy. The whole world was waiting for you. War, famine, and constant burning desires for love were ravaging the poor conditioned souls. But, now you have come. Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the greatest of all masters. You are the greatest of kings, poets and scholars. You are the greatest of generals. Your opulences have no limit. You give us shelter though we are filled with the bad qualities of anger, greed and envy. Without you our bodies become useless. We are simply animallike servants of illusion, accepting death again and again.

Please engage this insignificant soul in the service of Rādha and Kṛṣṇa. After being tortured for an eternity by the witch *Māyā*, we have finally accepted you as the captain of our ship. These bodies are yours. Let us help expand your mercy everywhere through the inadequate work of our own hands. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that you will be very strong and healthy as you surcharge this dark world with your transcendental energies. We want to give up our selfishness and serve you in cooperation. Please allow us to realize that by accepting the shelter of your lotus feet and worshiping you by preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness under the direction of the GBC and *sannyāsis*, we are perfectly situated in *your* service. In this spirit we can continue to help you spread this movement, and Lord Caitanya's name will be glorified in every town and village.

In Vṛndāvana you were living in one house before you moved into the Rādhā Dāmodara temple. The landlord showed us the spot where you sat, chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa and seeing the transcendental form of the Lord. At that time, in the Western world no one knew of Lord Caitanya, what to speak of following His instructions. Now, by your mercy, in almost every major city of the world, the conditioned souls can follow in your lotus footsteps by sitting before the Deity and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa. Thus you have expanded Vṛndāvana.

Please give us the strength to follow your bold example. O Gurudeva, purify our sinful hearts as we struggle to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this dark material world. By your mercy the hearts of the living entities who have all fallen under the influence of so many ism's will be conquered.

The devotees of ISKCON Sao Paulo

## Seattle

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda:*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel that you are our real father because you have given us spiritual birth. The shelter of your lotus feet is protection for us against the hellish existence of material life. By your ideal example and your patient and kind instruction you are teaching us everything. You are teaching us how to eat, how to clean ourselves, how to sleep, how to pass stool, how to dress, and most of all you are teaching us how to love. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your children, and it is by your mercy that we exist. You are taking us gently by the hand as helpless babes in the lap of *māyā* and teaching us to walk naturally in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is painful and frustrating for us, and we fall down, but you help us up with encouraging words. Prabhupāda, we are afraid that if you take away your helping hand now we will simply fall on our faces and not be able to get up. Please do not leave us like this!

Actually, we can understand that the relationship with the spiritual master is eternal and that he is always with his sincere disciple whether he remains on this earthly plane or not. The spiritual master lives not in a body but in his instructions. No one can say, Prabhupāda, that you have not given us in your books all the instructions necessary for us to go back to home, back to Godhead. Prabhupāda, we pray that we can somehow grow up and mature quickly so that we can follow these instructions without being such a burden to you!

Your menial servants of Seattle Yātrā,

## South Africa

*All glories to His All-merciful Divine Grace Oṁ Viṣnupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara Śata Śrī-Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Mahārāja Prabhupāda!!!*

Our dear lord and master, we offer our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Your Divine Grace has been sent to every corner of this planet in order to save the poor conditioned souls from the miseries of *māyā*. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is described how Kali was banished to live in places where gold is hoarded. Here in South Africa this is the largest industry. They are all working like *mūḍhas* to acquire this yellow stool. Perhaps in no other country on the planet do these lost souls labor under the bodily conception of life to the degree that they do in South Africa. Those with different color bodies have to live in separate areas, go to different schools, ride in separate buses, sit on different park benches; the list is almost endless. Baffled by *māyā*, frustrated at every step by illusion, they desperately take shelter of intoxication and sense gratification.

Yet even this capital of Kali on the furthest edge of the dark continent has been benedicted by the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace. When you visited South Africa, thousands came to the program to hear the ambrosial nectar and transcendental knowledge presented by Your Divine Grace, and we pray that you may bless us with another such visit. Out of your endless compassion, countless numbers of lost souls have been drawn into the net of your mercy by reading Your Divine Grace's writings, tasting the *prasāda* offered to the Lord, becoming life members of ISKCON, and hearing and chanting the names of Kṛṣṇa.

We, your unqualified and undistinguished servants, know that there is no way for us to repay the blessings you have heaped upon us. For the most part, we are simply the sitting places of lust, envy, greed and all bad qualities. By your mercy only we have been raised out of darkness, delusion and suffering and shown the light of transcendental knowledge. Therefore on this Vyāsa-pūjā day we beg you to accept this insignificant offering, even though we are so selfish and full of envy.

May we always remain your humble servants,

## St. Louis

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master delivers his devotees. "One should know the *ācārya* as Myself and never disrespect him in any way." The bona fide spiritual master always engages in unalloyed devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead. By this test he is known to be a direct manifestation of the Lord and a genuine representative of Śrī Nityananda Prabhu.

When the deluded spirit soul enters the material world to make his feeble attempt to reject Kṛṣṇa, the compassionate Supreme Lord in the form of Balarāma or Nityānanda expands Himself through various forms down to the Supersoul within the heart. This Baladeva-Nitāi is known as the original spiritual master; as the guru within the heart, *caitya-guru*, He attempts to guide the living entity back to the spiritual world. But when the soul becomes eternally addicted to material sense enjoyment, the impartial Supersoul fulfills his unlimited material desires according to his *karma*, and the soul can no longer directly hear the Supersoul asking him to go back to home. For these *nitya-baddha* souls, however, Balarāma again manifests Himself in the external form of the bona fide guru. The *ācārya*, or pure devotee, has a different position from the Supersoul in that he can interfere with the conditioned soul who is misusing his marginal independence to reject Kṛṣṇa. The spiritual master is not sworn to impartiality like the Supersoul.

Only out of His immense compassion does the Personality of Godhead reveal Himself as the spiritual master. He is the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead. It is worthwhile to take shelter of such a steady devotee, who is called *āśraya-vigraha*, or the manifestation or form of the Lord of whom one must take shelter. This is also confirmed by Śrīla Viśvanatha Cakravartī Thākura: by the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you explain in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* that it is Nārada Muni only who can deliver the path of *bhakti-yoga*: *nāra* means the Supreme Lord and *da* means one who can deliver. But you are the bona fide *ācārya* in the succession from Nārada: therefore now it is you only who can deliver Kṛṣṇa to the fallen soul.

Everyone in this horrible place is afflicted with the jaundice of *avidyā*, ignorance, and they cannot find the sweet taste of real spiritual happiness. But you have brought the sweet, transcendental sugar candy from Goloka in the form of Kṛṣṇa's name, form, qualities, pastimes and devotional service, and as the supreme doctor, you are trying to spoon-feed this medicine to all the fallen souls in order to cure their diseased condition. That sweet taste is found in its pure form in the great sound incarnations of God like *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and *Śrīmad Bhagavad-gitā*. So under the order of your spiritual master, you are dedicating your life to translating these books and explaining them to the entire universe.

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you explain that these transcendental sounds vibrating on your tongue are directly the saffron dust particles coming from the Lord's lotus feet. You also explain that the dust of the lotus feet of the spiritual master is his instructions, and that these instructions should be the life and soul of the disciple. So every day we are singing: *guru-mukha-padma-vakya, cittete koriyā aikya ār nā koriho mane āśā—*our only wish is to have our consciousness purified by the words emanating from your lotus mouth. Therefore we must surrender to all of your instructions to make this wish completely true. Now we are foolishly taking instruction from our minds, whose message is the sum total of all the bogus gurus that we've followed since time immemorial (*mano-rathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ,* SB 5.18.12). But you explain our real duty in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*: "One must accept a spiritual master who comes in the disciplic succession, and under his direction, one must then engage his three propensities, namely his body, mind and words. The body should be engaged in physical activity under the order of the master, the mind should think of Kṛṣṇa incessantly, and one's words should be engaged in preaching the glories of the Lord. If one is thus engaged in the loving service of the Lord, one's life is successful." In this way you are always canvassing on Kṛṣṇa's behalf—*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru  \[*Bg. 18.65\]*.*

Lord Caitanya preached *prema pumartho mahān*—the goal of human life is to develop pure love for God. And in Detroit you said, "*Guru-Kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*: And to approach guru means just to beg Kṛṣṇa from Him." But we do not know what to ask from you. You are the desire tree which can give your disciple whatever he wants. But we are so fallen that we are asking for broken glass instead of the real gem that you possess: pure, unalloyed devotional service. Associating with you through your instructions, *vāṇī*, gives us the vision by which we can leave the dark forest of material enjoyment and obtain the Lord's shelter. In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, you explain that a devotee who follows the instructions of the Lord approaches Him (*mām eti*), and as a spiritual person, such a devotee returns to the Personality of Godhead and plays and dances with Him. So actually surrendering to all of your instructions makes us eligible for more and more mercy from *Kṛṣṇa- yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādaḥ.*

The disciple must be eager to follow the spiritual master's orders. "To those who are constantly devoted and worship Me with love, I give the understanding by which they can come to Me." *\[*Bg. 10.10\]This is very practical. A teacher instructs the student if the student is capable of taking more and more instructions. Otherwise, in spite of being instructed by the teacher, the student cannot make strides in his understanding. This indicates that Kṛṣṇa is prepared to give *bhakti-yoga* to everyone, but one must be capable of receiving it. That is the secret. So every day we are singing: *śrī-guru-caraṇe rati*—attachment to your lotus feet is the perfection of life. We must become steadfast in our determination to follow every instruction you have given us.

You always try to enliven us about Kṛṣṇa, which increases our desire to serve you, because serving you is the only way to reach that Supreme Lord. You explain this in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*: "If one seriously accepts the lotus feet of the Lord, he is purified by the grace of the Lord and by the grace of the Lord's servant. One who is brought under the lotus feet of the Lord by the endeavor of the Lord's servant, the spiritual master, is certainly immediately purified, however lowborn he may be. He becomes eligible to return home, back to Godhead. This is the sign of associating with great devotees. A devotee takes instruction from a liberated soul and is thus overwhelmed with ecstasy from transcendental pleasure."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you explain in the *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu* that association with a pure devotee can have two effects. If it is properly executed, even a *mleccha* can be elevated to the platform of *paramahaṁsa*. But if offenses are committed against a pure devotee, then one's attachment to Kṛṣṇa can be reduced to practically nothing. So we are begging that you sanction our feeble attempt to follow your instructions. Please don't let us fall away from your service because of our offenses. If you examine us, you will find no good qualities. Your mercy is all that we are made of. If you are not merciful unto us, we can only cry hopelessly.

*Your fallen servants,*

## Sydney

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this blessed day  
Our humble obeisances unto you we pay  
And with folded hands we fervently pray  
For Kṛṣṇa to protect you always.

For your mission is surely the greatest of all  
To rescue those souls who've decided to fall  
From Kṛṣṇa's service to follow māyā's call  
And take birth in this horrible place.

This material world is a deep dark well  
Where everyone gradually is gliding to hell  
Where sight, sound, touch, taste and smell  
Are our masters so impersonal and cruel.

You have appeared to give us direction  
How to become free from this hellish connection  
From material life you are giving us protection  
We were drowning in this material pool!

Your compassion for us is unflinching and sweet  
Where would we be without your lotus feet?  
Simply no better than dogs in the street  
Looking for shelter somewhere.

So you have come to show us the way  
How to perfect our human life in every way  
To approach the goal and never sway  
This is your mercy so rare.

Many are the gurus who come to steal our wealth  
They say "give me money, I'll give you better sex and good health'  
But rare is that guru who can actually help  
Our miserable material condition.

So many gurus have come and gone  
They may juggle some words but what have they done?  
Some magic tricks perhaps, but factually no one  
Has been found to improve his position.

But since Your Divine Grace has come to save the West  
So many people have by your mercy been blessed  
With the strength to conquer Māyādevī's test  
And advance in spiritual life.

By your preaching work, destroying the Kali-yuga dream  
You are deprogramming the world from dirty brain to clean  
You are the greatest revolutionary the world has ever seen  
Uprooting Kali's quarrel and strife.

Certainly no one but you has been so assured  
That the diseased condition of the world can be cured  
So many troubles for this end you've endured  
To save the world from disaster.

You are jagad-guru, the savior of all  
Mercifully spreading hari-nāma you walk so tall  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the greatest and we are small.  
Please eternally remain our lord and master.

Jaya Gurudeva, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande paramānanda-mādhavam*  
*Cc. Madhya 17.80*

**"By the mercy of the guru even a dumb man can become the greatest orator and even a lame man can cross over mountains."**

You tell the story of the wild tiger in the jungle who was harassing the members of an *āśrama* as they travelled to and from the temple on the jungle path. In an attempt to appease the hungry beast, the head of the *āśrama* placed a large bowl of milk out for the tiger each day without fail. As time passed, the satisfied animal, being freed from the anxiety of having to search out his own food, began to think to himself, "Yes, this man loves me," so he could understand the motive of the *āśrama* head and did not bother the devotees any more.

Similarly, each day you give us such beautiful *prasāda* and ask for nothing in return. Surely one day our dull, animallike intelligence will realize, "Yes, this person loves us," and we will serve your lotus feet with grateful enthusiasm.

Kṛṣṇa and the *dhāma* of Goloka are not our real goal. Just to become your real disciples and to relish your pastimes is the direction given in the Vedas (*Padma Purāṇa*):

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

Lord Śiva tells Pārvati that of all types of worship, worship of Viṣṇu is the highest, but higher still is the worship of His devotees.

The light of the Vedas will reveal you to this darkened world; the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is but a facet of your character. The *Vedas* sing your glories and all pure devotees throughout all creation realize your exalted position. Your fame is not to be limited to this small planet, and your power is given by Kṛṣṇa Himself. Your accepting us as disciples is an obvious display of your causeless mercy.

Let us realize that our position is only through the flow of your unceasing compassion upon us and all souls.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the strength to bow our heads in the path of your universal march of all beings back to Godhead.

## Trinidad

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Our merciful lord, we are trying to the best of our ability to glorify your divine mercy, which is impossible due to a lack of purity. We cannot perfectly glorify thee, but because of your potency, by endeavoring, one day we will become purified. The only thing we can say is what you taught us. You are so magnanimous that the only way you can be satisfied is by your own benevolent activities, which involve delivering souls from the clutches of *māyā*, which is a matchless gift for which we can never repay thee. Thinking to repay our beloved master and lord is *vinoda-pātra* (joking).

Please allow us to serve you eternally, and kindly accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*Your Most Fallen Servants* at ISKCON Trinidad
